# maxio · 项目状态（STATUS）

> 单一事实来源（cross-session / 抗闪退）。最后更新：2026-07-14。
> 设计 spec：`docs/superpowers/specs/2026-06-08-maxio-s3-foundation-design.md`（+ `_design_inputs/`）
> 工程准则：`AGENTS.md`（先读）；Claude/OMC 执行细节：`CLAUDE.md`

## 项目目标

用 Elixir/Phoenix 完整复刻 MinIO 的全功能、Amazon S3 兼容对象存储系统（aws-cli/boto3/mc/SDK 直连）。工程约束参考姊妹项目寰析（huanxi）。

## Latest Batch Update (2026-07-14 ObjectMutation P1-CLOSEOUT-A / full-gate closeout)

- **P1-CLOSEOUT-A 已完成，但 ObjectMutation Phase 1 总闭环仍未完成。** 普通 PG-authority fresh Put 在 `metadata_committed` 禁止线前同步失败或 kill+recovery 回滚时，endpoint 只在 durable epoch 为 exact `{generation, mutation_id}`、phase 合法且 manifest 精确为 `entries=[] + previous_token=nil` 后，才在同一 `EpochStore` worker 串行临界区幂等删除本 mutation 发布的 final；随后写 `rolled_back` epoch 并清 backup。FS/EC/Distributed、Commit、Recovery 与真实 Bandit/SigV4 用例均直接断言 PG/API 不可见、journal/epoch `rolled_back`、backup 清空且盘上 final **物理不存在**；overwrite 仍逐字恢复旧 bytes/sidecar，successor、epoch/manifest 损坏及 quorum 不足继续 fail-closed。该项关闭了下方 2026-07-11 遗留清单中的 “fresh-put rollback 已发布字节留盘”，不再把它推给 Phase 2C 读屏障。
- `Maxio.Storage.EpochStore.Worker` 已从 `epoch_store.ex` 等价拆到 `lib/maxio/storage/epoch_store/worker.ex`，一文件一模块；模块名、PartitionSupervisor child spec/name、分片路由 key、30 秒 call timeout 与运行拓扑不变。实现经独立初审与 Minor 修订后的独立复审双审批准；整批最终独立审查对 P1-CLOSEOUT-A 及下述三项 full-suite 根因修复给出 `SPEC COMPLIANCE: APPROVED`、`CODE QUALITY: APPROVED`、无 Critical/Important/Minor。
- 第一次完整门禁真实失败为 `3660 tests, 3 failures, 5 excluded`，三处互相独立的根因已按架构修复并独立复审：(1) InFlight `BlockingBackend` 补全 `Maxio.Storage.Backend` behaviour，并把未拦截能力和 mutation callbacks 委托真实 FS，barrier 只控制真实 publish closure 时序；(2) Ops Compat heterogeneous blocker/warning row identity 改为稳定的 `kind + source + source-local ordinal`，显示字段不再充当 DOM 主键且结构化 reason 可安全展示；(3) DriveDeviceIO CLI 测试在已注册 owner 内写 cache，并用 `:sys.suspend/replace_state/resume` + `on_exit` 兜底建立单 writer 临界区，不关闭生产周期 refresh、不改变 telemetry 语义。
- 最终可信 full gate 由全新独立 verification lane **只运行一次**：PowerShell 5.1 不直接承接 native stderr，而由 `cmd.exe` 内部执行 `call mix.bat precommit > log 2>&1`，cmd 返回后立即把真实 `$LASTEXITCODE` 写入 ASCII sidecar；持久 log/sidecar 证明 real Mix exit `0`，唯一最终摘要为 **3665 tests, 0 failures, 5 excluded**，`git diff --check` exit `0`。Windows 教训：PowerShell 5.1 的 native stderr 配合 `$ErrorActionPreference='Stop'`/EAP Stop 可能让捕获器在命令后的证据捕获逻辑执行前终止，导致真实 exit/摘要未持久化；完整门禁必须采用 cmd 自重定向 + 独立 exit sidecar 交叉验证，不能以截断终端输出代替退出码。
- 最终门禁时间窗内 93 个 `%TEMP%\\maxio*` 顶层目录经独立审计逐项证明归属本轮测试（227,964 bytes、104 files、69 nested dirs、零 reparse point），随后只按 93 条解析绝对路径 allowlist 精确清理：deleted 93 / remaining 0 / failed 0；allowlist 外 5,325 项前后全量指纹一致，未触碰工作区、build、artifact、客户端配置、数据库或既有 `phx.server`。
- **状态漂移校正与仍开放边界：** B1–B7 的 journal/schema/context、三后端 epoch CAS、普通 PutObject Commit/recovery、CLI/日志与 external matrix 已实现，不能再写成“仅 contract slice、B1–B7 尚未实现”；但后续架构审计重新打开了两个 Phase 1 blocking remediation，因此也不能继续写成“Phase 1 已全部收口”：(a) finalize 前读取并校验 final 实际 fingerprint，不符时保留 backup 并 retry/quarantine；(b) Distributed 在任何 claim IO 前把 frozen placement snapshot durable 写入 prepared journal，关闭 claim quorum → journal placement snapshot 的 crash window。计划中其余尚未完成的 Phase 1 项继续保持开放。`Multipart.complete` 的 `Storage.put`/`Storage.assemble` 分支明确留在 Phase 2A；multipart part/abort、delete、metadata-only 及其后续 read barrier/heal/rebalance/GC 仍按 Phase 2B/2C 推进。本批不得外推为 ObjectMutation 全局写路径闭环、Copy/Multipart/delete crash-safe、MinIO 全兼容或 production-ready。

## Latest Batch Update (2026-07-11 ObjectMutation Phase 2A C4 AccessLog / Console single put seam 迁移)

- Phase 2A 进行中批次记录（总收口口径与跨后端矩阵归 C9，此处不作任何"Phase 2A 完成"声明）。依据 `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-11-object-mutation-phase2a-impl.md` C4 节：两个无锁 caller——AccessLog delivery（`lib/maxio/s3/plugs/access_log_delivery.ex` `write_log_object/5`，operation `"generated_put"`）与 Console single-object upload（`lib/maxio/console/object_upload.ex` `upload_single_put/6`，operation `"put"`）——迁入 `Maxio.ObjectMutation.Commit` seam：`around_object_commit` 回调内取 `Lock.with_object_lock`（do_put 同构，D5 补锁），metadata_tx = `Objects.put_version_with_id` + `Commit.transaction_hook(journal)`（journal `metadata_committed` 与 PG row 同一事务）；原 publish 后 plain `Storage.delete` 回滚点（`cleanup_log_object` 全函数、`cleanup_uploaded_object` 的 single-put 调用分支）删除；`:sidecar` 写权威分支维持非协议路径（D1，协议化归 2B；console 无 best-effort index 分支，维持其既有致命 PG 提交值语义）。quota 检查按 do_put 同构前置到锁内、claim/publish 之前（fail-closed：零 journal、无孤儿字节；publish 后再失败只能 seam restore；此段当时记录的 fresh-key rolled_back 字节留盘遗留已由 2026-07-14 P1-CLOSEOUT-A 改为恢复物理 absence）。
- **D2 遗留（正确性优先，照常 journal 化，禁止采样/旁路）**：access log delivery 的 journal 写放大——每个启用 BucketLogging 的请求 +1 journal 行生命周期（1 INSERT + ~4 次 phase UPDATE）+ 每 endpoint 2 次 epoch CAS fsync。缓解：(a) 该 feature 按桶 opt-in；(b) 正确的性能出路是 AWS 原生行为的**批量聚合投递重设计**（产品行为变更，非 2A 范围，留待独立规划）；(c) journal 终态行 GC 归 Phase 2C GC 范畴。
- 测试基础设施发现（既有行为，非本次引入）：`Maxio.Cluster.Lock.wait_for_result` 对持锁期间发到 caller 邮箱的非匹配消息有 catch-all 吞噬分支，且锁内 fun 在 spawn_monitor worker 进程执行——锁内向测试进程 send 的断言手段不可用，C4 锁存在性测试改用 Agent 同步收集探针结果（零 sleep）。
- 验证（executor 侧聚焦门，独立复审另行执行）：`mix compile --warnings-as-errors` 通过；新建 `test/maxio/s3/access_log_mutation_test.exs` + `test/maxio/console/object_upload_mutation_test.exs` → 10 tests, 0 failures（五位一体快乐路径、metadata 失败保旧目标 + AccessLog best-effort 原请求 200、console quota fail-closed、锁存在性构造断言、sidecar gate 零 seam 参与）；既有聚焦回归 `object_upload_test.exs` + `logging_e2e_test.exs`（含两条 quota best-effort 用例零改动通过）+ `write_authority_test.exs` → 35 tests, 0 failures；相邻 seam 回归（object_writer/report/copy mutation + commit_test）→ 32 tests, 0 failures；scoped `mix format --check-formatted` 通过。`object_upload_test.exs` 中钉住已删除 plain-delete 机制的单条用例（single put + 注入 delete 失败后断言 cleanup 失败日志与字节残留）改写为 C4 后契约：quota 预检在 publish 前 fail-closed，delete 后端注入失败也零孤儿字节。

## Latest Batch Update (2026-07-11 ObjectMutation Phase 1 普通 PutObject PG 写权威 durable recovery closeout)

- 按已批准协议 `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-10-object-mutation-protocol.md` 与实现计划 `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-11-object-mutation-phase1-impl.md`，B1–B7 七个串行 executor 批次完成了 Phase 1 的基础已实施子集（协议步骤 1–6 的前置批），每批经独立 code-reviewer 审查 `APPROVED`；这只代表当时范围内的基线通过验收，不代表 ObjectMutation Phase 1 总闭环，后续开放 blockers/TODO 仍须关闭。**允许的完成口径严格限于协议 §6**：普通 PutObject 的 PG 写权威路径已全程 journal 化（prepared→claimed→published→metadata_committed→finalize_pending→finalized，journal `metadata_committed` 与 Objects/outbox/quota 同一 PG 事务），FS/ErasureCoded/Distributed 三后端的 durable recovery 路径（同步 metadata 失败、owner/publisher DOWN、lease lost、PG commit 后 kill、finalize failure、successor fence、quarantine 决策表）经聚焦与端到端测试通过。
- 落地组件：`Maxio.Storage.EpochStore`（节点本地串行 epoch 临界区，`{generation, mutation_id}` fence 全序 CAS，`nil/:enoent` 绝不当授权）；`.maxio-object-mutations/<mutation_id>/` durable backup + manifest；`Maxio.Failpoint`；三后端 `claim_epoch/current_epoch/restore_if_current/finalize_if_current`（EC/Distributed 按 `epoch_quorum = max(write_quorum, ⌊n/2⌋+1)` 多数派钳制，两个 quorum 必有交集；Distributed 恢复端点只认 journal placement snapshot，漂移 → quarantine）；`Maxio.ObjectMutation.Commit` around-mutation seam；`Maxio.ObjectMutation.Recovery` + Scheduler（生产默认 enabled 的 correctness 组件）。
- B7 收口：CLI `mix maxio.object_mutations.status`（journal phase / backend epoch quorum 逐 endpoint 读数 / placement snapshot / backup manifest 存在性 / 建议动作，默认只读；attempts 超阈值的持久 partial 给出操作员裁决口径）与 `mix maxio.object_mutations.recover`（默认 dry-run 零副作用；`--execute` 与 recovery worker 同一 `Recovery.recover/2`/`drain/1` 入口禁止旁路；`--older-than` 落到 `list_due` 的 `older_than_ms`）。协议 §7 固定日志字段（mutation_id/mutation_generation/mutation_operation/mutation_phase/authority_mode/backend/placement_set_id/outcome）进入 logger `:default_formatter` metadata 白名单并有测试断言日志行真实携带；`Objects.Object`/`Multipart.Upload` 的 `sse_envelope` 加 schema 级 `redact: true`（inspect/reason 通道不泄露密钥材料）；handler 对协议路径 journal metadata CAS 争用（stale_journal）补 503 SlowDownWrite 结构化映射 + 端到端断言；`.tmp` 残留断言的 Windows `Path.wildcard` 假覆盖 idiom 统一改 `File.ls` 判空；recovery 锁内收敛失败分支补主动释放租约；Scheduler 定时自动 drain 路径补测试。
- 端到端证据（真实 Bandit socket）：普通签名 PUT 与 `STREAMING-AWS4-HMAC-SHA256` aws-chunked PUT 的五位一体断言（bytes/sidecar/PG row/journal finalized/epoch committed 同一 token + fingerprint）；failpoint `:kill_self` 驱动的两条 crash-recover e2e——PG commit 后 kill 收敛 finalized 且**新字节保留**、metadata 前 kill 收敛 rolled_back 且**旧字节逐字恢复**。聚焦验证：recovery+fs 44 tests / 0 failures，CLI 10 / 0，e2e 7 / 0，相邻回归（journal contract、Commit seam、ObjectCommit、Bandit put、streaming matrix、SSE e2e、multipart integration、CLI）125 / 0；完整 `mix precommit` 见下条。
- 完整 `mix precommit`（Phase 1 全程唯一一次）：3596 tests, 0 failures, 5 excluded。
- **不得声明**（协议 §6 明令）：ObjectMutation 已覆盖全部对象写入；CopyObject、Multipart、delete、metadata-only 已 crash-safe；heal/rebalance 与 tombstone 已闭环；MinIO 全兼容或 production-ready。
- aws-cli/mc/boto3 活体矩阵已由独立 verifier 在隔离环境收口（协议 §6 external-client closeout 验收达成）：启动日志证明 heal/rebalance/scanner/scrubber/lifecycle 全部 disabled（唯一 enabled 的 recovery scheduler 是 Phase 1 协议自身 correctness 组件）。主 artifact `artifacts/s3_compat_matrix_external_phase1.json` gate_status=ok，6/6（aws-cli 12/12 步、mc 10/10 步、boto3 deep matrix ok）；补充 List 覆盖 `artifacts/s3_compat_matrix_external_phase1_list_supplement.json` 三客户端各 7/7（create→put→head→get→list→delete_object→delete_bucket）。aws-chunked 活体覆盖由 mc 提供（`--debug` 抓到 `STREAMING-AWS4-HMAC-SHA256-PAYLOAD` + `X-Amz-Decoded-Content-Length`，服务器 200）；**行为差异如实记录**：aws-cli 2.35.20 对 http 端点默认预计算 `x-amz-checksum-crc64nvme` + 全量 payload SHA256 签名（botocore 仅对 https 走 aws-chunked），两种真实签名模式服务器均正确接受，已写入 artifact `aws_chunked_coverage` 字段。收口检查：测试桶/temp/临时 mc config 残留 0、`~/mc`/`~/.mc` 不存在、artifact 零 credential。操作序列 runbook 已持久化：`docs/superpowers/runbooks/object_mutation_phase1_external_matrix.md`。新发现（既有行为，非本次引入）：FS 后端 DeleteBucket 留空目录壳（API 不可见），2026-07-10 先例运行的 12 个既有空壳仍在 `priv/s3_data/dev`，后续独立处理。
- 显式遗留（不作静默假设，按协议分期处置）：
  - fresh-put rollback 后已发布字节留盘这一遗留已于 **2026-07-14 P1-CLOSEOUT-A 关闭**：exact-token/phase/manifest 授权下恢复盘上物理 absence；不再推给 Phase 2C 读屏障；
  - EC 少数盘 successor token 在 restore 全局裁决中折叠为整体 `:superseded` 且返回值不含 successor token——recovery 另经 `current_epoch` 取证，少数盘取证边界归 Phase 2C；
  - EC 少数盘 claim/backup 残留可造成 restore 永久 `{:partial,_}` 可重试不收敛（fail-safe 零破坏、backup 保留）——操作员经 CLI status 的 attempts 超阈值裁决口径处置，收敛归 Phase 2C heal；
  - 盘移除 quarantine 的 move_layout_roots 只迁 buckets/.versions/.uploads，epoch 与 backup 不随迁——Phase 2C 显式决策；
  - sidecar 写权威（`authoritative_source(:write)=:sidecar`）的 put 未协议化，维持既有非协议路径（其 journal 状态机 sidecar published 禁回滚已就位）——Phase 2A/2B 与 Copy/Multipart 同批迁移；
  - backup manifest 直写无 temp+rename（半写 manifest 由 decode 结构化拒绝，fail-closed 无数据风险）——Phase 2C 噪声收口。

## Latest Batch Update (2026-07-10 External clients / DeleteObjects / ObjectMutation contract closeout)

- 本机已安装并实际验证官方 AWS CLI `2.35.20`、MinIO Client `mc RELEASE.2025-08-13` 与 boto3 `1.43.45`；`mc` 二进制 SHA-256 为 `c8db13ebeda31497f354c0e950809db0ae9b2a2a69b8afee68c128c37300c157`。验证过程不持久化 AWS credentials；`mc` 每次使用独立临时 config，完成后清理，不写入用户默认配置。
- External smoke harness 已修复 loopback endpoint 的 `NO_PROXY` / `no_proxy` 合并去重与 metadata attribute 参数兼容，远端 endpoint 不注入 loopback bypass。最终证据为 `artifacts/s3_compat_matrix_external_final6.json`：external clients `6/6`，AWS CLI 12 steps、`mc` 10 steps、boto3 deep matrix 全部通过；收口检查确认 buckets/objects/configs/temp 残留均为 `0`、`~/mc` 不存在，artifact 中无 credential/secret。
- 真实 `mc rm` 触发的 multi-delete 请求确认发送 `<Quiet>false</Quiet>`；Maxio 的 DeleteObjects parser 已兼容该客户端 XML，专项验证 `15 tests, 0 failures`，独立复审 `APPROVED`。
- PutObject body-owner 问题已通过 request owner 完整 staging 后再进入 `ObjectCommit` 的路径修复，Bandit 普通签名 PUT、ObjectCommit 聚焦回归与 external client matrix 均提供活体证据。正式架构审查进一步推动并批准 `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-10-object-mutation-protocol.md`，避免继续用局部 rollback callback 扩张到其它 mutator。
- **截至 2026-07-10 当批**，ObjectMutation 只完成第一阶段的 journal/schema/context 与 ObjectEpoch target/token/fingerprint/encoding/path contract slice：官方 generator 生成 `object_mutations` migration，dev/test migration up/down/re-migrate 通过，聚焦 `29 tests, 0 failures`，独立多轮 review 最终 `APPROVED`。这是历史时间点快照：后续 B1–B7 已实现，不应再把 backend CAS、PutObject orchestration、recovery/CLI 写成当前未实现；但 2026-07-14 状态仍有 final fingerprint gate 与 Distributed prepared-journal placement durability 等 Phase 1 remediation 未完成，且 CopyObject、Multipart、delete、metadata-only、heal、rebalance 仍不得宣称 crash-safe/全局闭环。
- `bucket_configs` 已补 bucket-name foreign key，既有 orphan fixtures 被收口；删除 bucket 时相关 config cascade 清理，迁移与测试覆盖 orphan/cascade 语义。
- 完整 `mix precommit` 首轮暴露 7 个失败，分别由三个独立实现/调试 lane 按根因修复；最终门禁为 **3489 tests, 0 failures, 5 excluded**。External smoke、DeleteObjects、ObjectMutation、bucket-config fixture/FK 与 full-suite 修复均经职责隔离的独立 review，最终结论均为 `APPROVED`。

## Latest Batch Update (2026-07-10 P2 Compat Longtail A-F semantic audit / full verification closeout)

- Closed coherent milestone `P2-COMPAT-LONGTAIL-CLOSEOUT-01`. This milestone jointly audited and fully verified the previously scoped A-F compatibility slices instead of adding another LONGTAIL-G feature before returning the deferred full-suite verification debt.
- Hardened Restore SELECT semantics against the AWS S3 RestoreObject SELECT contract: the source now requires `s3:GetObject` before staging/reading bytes in addition to `s3:RestoreObject`, target `s3:PutObject` authorization remains fail-closed, and `x-amz-restore-output-path` now returns a random trailing-slash job prefix while the result object is written below it as `part-00000`.
- Corrected Object Lock authorization and transitions. Explicit `versionId` reads keep requiring `s3:GetObjectRetention` / `s3:GetObjectLegalHold` rather than being downgraded to `s3:GetObjectVersion`; active GOVERNANCE and COMPLIANCE retention may be extended without bypass; active GOVERNANCE shortening/removal still requires the existing header + `s3:BypassGovernanceRetention` double gate; active COMPLIANCE shortening/removal/mode change remains denied.
- Closed sidecar-authority safety gaps found by independent follow-up review. Object Lock write validation now reads the complete object from the same authority used for the write, merges partial retention/legal-hold updates with the authoritative three-field lock state, and prevents stale PG indexes from weakening COMPLIANCE. Sidecar reads distinguish true `:enoent` from endpoint/quorum failure, so only pure missing maps to not-found while unavailable/mixed insufficient quorum maps to read-quorum/SlowDown. Derived PG index error/raise/throw after a durable sidecar commit is logged as best-effort without converting authoritative success into an ambiguous 500; authoritative sidecar read/write failures are still fail-closed.
- Hardened Object Detail LiveView integration. Latest delete markers no longer invoke the object-tagging API as if a live current object existed; tag editing is hidden and forged tag events fail closed while historical versions remain browsable. Deleting the final normal version or final delete marker now navigates back to bucket browse instead of retaining stale detail/tag/Object Lock state. Console tests were aligned with AWS semantics that retention extension is allowed and legal hold is independent from retention changes, while active shortening/clear without bypass remains covered as denied.
- Independent review proceeded through multiple fix/review waves. The initial combined review found 2 High + 2 Medium issues; sidecar follow-up found 2 High, then 2 Medium; LiveView follow-up found one final Medium. Each was repaired by an implementation lane and re-reviewed by a separate read-only lane. Final review returned `APPROVED` with no High/Medium/Low findings.
- Verification evidence: Restore/Object Lock focused -> 51 tests, 0 failures; sidecar authority/quorum focused -> 61 tests, 0 failures; Object Detail/Tagging/Object Lock combined -> 87 tests, 0 failures; final Object Detail focused -> 49 tests, 0 failures; repeated `MIX_ENV=test mix compile --warnings-as-errors --no-deps-check`, targeted format checks, and `git diff --check` passed. The first full closeout run exposed 8 stale/LiveView failures (`3427 tests, 8 failures, 5 excluded`) and produced no worktree changes; after root-cause fixes, final `mix precommit` passed with **3429 tests, 0 failures, 5 excluded**. Compile, unused-dependency unlock, format, and test stages all passed, and pre/post worktree fingerprints were identical.
- Residual `VERIFY-AT-IMPL` items are explicit rather than silently guessed: Requester Pays generic-guide wording conflicts with per-operation API/SDK models for SelectObjectContent/DeleteObjectTagging; equal-date PutObjectRetention and GOVERNANCE-to-COMPLIANCE transitions still need live AWS/MinIO evidence. Real aws-cli/mc/boto3 validation remains blocked because those clients are not installed in the current environment.
- Current completion reading: A-F are now a coherently verified compatibility milestone. The whole MinIO clone remains incomplete; next planning should choose LONGTAIL-G or release/production work after considering the still-blocked real-client lane, broader low-frequency API/XML differences, production multi-node/fault-injection evidence, release/deploy/runbooks, and external KMS/HSM readiness.

## Latest Batch Update (2026-07-05 P2 Compat Longtail Object Tagging / Object ACL Subresource Guard closeout)

- Closed scoped slice `P2-COMPAT-LONGTAIL-F` for Object Tagging and Object ACL subresource guard hardening. This slice intentionally did not expand bucket tagging/ACL behavior, directory bucket/access point/Outposts/email-grantee behavior, lifecycle/replication/copy/MPU behavior, schema/model shape, real aws-cli/mc/boto3 external smoke, production multi-node work, or release documentation; full `mix precommit` remains deferred to a larger compatibility milestone.
- Hardened object tagging version semantics through `Objects.get_tags_with_version/3` and `Objects.put_tags_with_version/4`. `GetObjectTagging`, `PutObjectTagging`, and `DeleteObjectTagging` now return the actual target version id and use version-specific IAM actions when `versionId` is present.
- Hardened delete-marker subresource behavior after independent review. Tagging operations no longer read or mutate tag metadata on delete-marker rows, and `GetObjectAcl` no longer renders ACL XML for delete markers. Current delete markers return `404` with `x-amz-delete-marker: true` and the marker version id when non-null; explicit delete-marker `versionId` requests return `405` with `x-amz-delete-marker: true`, `last-modified`, and the marker version id when non-null.
- Added focused E2E coverage in `test/maxio/s3/tagging_e2e_test.exs` for current and explicit delete-marker `GetObjectTagging`, `PutObjectTagging`, and `DeleteObjectTagging`, including proof that failed tagging subresource calls do not mutate the delete-marker row's tags. Added `test/maxio/s3/acl_test.exs` coverage for current and explicit delete-marker `GetObjectAcl` while proving the older live object version remains ACL-readable.
- Existing F-slice coverage also includes Object Tagging / Object ACL Requester Pays guard cases, signed/query payer charged headers, owner bypass, wrong expected-owner no-mutation behavior, versionId targeting, version-specific IAM action gates, ACL version response headers, and BucketOwnerEnforced ACL read/write behavior.
- Verification evidence: baseline focused `mix test test/maxio/s3/tagging_e2e_test.exs test/maxio/s3/acl_test.exs test/maxio/s3/request_payment_e2e_test.exs --max-failures 1` -> 50 tests, 0 failures; adjacent Tagging/ACL/RequesterPays/OwnershipControls/ExpectedOwner/Authz/Versioning -> 101 tests, 0 failures; delete-marker follow-up `mix test test/maxio/s3/tagging_e2e_test.exs test/maxio/s3/acl_test.exs` -> 34 tests, 0 failures; `MIX_ENV=test mix compile --warnings-as-errors --no-deps-check` passed; direct formatter equivalence passed; scoped `git diff --check` passed.
- Independent planning and review used separate local Codex read-only lanes, not Claude. Planning returned `APPROVED PLAN: keep P2-COMPAT-LONGTAIL-F Object Tagging + Object ACL subresource Requester Pays / expected-owner / version / ObjectOwnership guard matrix`. Initial review found one Medium delete-marker subresource issue; the implementation lane fixed it and added regressions. Follow-up review returned `Findings: None`, with residual non-blocking gaps around suspended `null` delete-marker variants and direct helper-level `put_acl_version/6` misuse outside the inspected handler path.

## Latest Batch Update (2026-07-05 P2 Compat Longtail Object Lock Requester Pays / Governance Guard closeout)

- Closed scoped slice `P2-COMPAT-LONGTAIL-E` for Object Lock object-level Requester Pays and governance guard hardening. This slice intentionally did not implement directory bucket/access point behavior, did not add a new Object Lock data model, did not rewrite Bucket ObjectLockConfiguration, did not run real aws-cli/mc/boto3 external smoke, did not touch production multi-node or release documentation, and deferred full `mix precommit` to a larger compat milestone.
- Added focused E2E coverage in `test/maxio/s3/object_lock_e2e_test.exs` for `PutObjectRetention`, `GetObjectRetention`, `PutObjectLegalHold`, and `GetObjectLegalHold` on Requester Pays object-lock-enabled buckets. A non-owner IAM principal is denied for missing payer, invalid signed payer, and unsigned payer header, succeeds and receives `x-amz-request-charged: requester` for signed-header or query payer acknowledgements, and bucket-owner calls without payer acknowledgement remain successful and uncharged.
- Added version-targeting coverage for object lock writes. Requester-paid retention and legal-hold updates with explicit `versionId` are proven to affect only the requested version: one version receives retention while the other remains without retention, and one version receives legal hold while the other remains `OFF`.
- Extended governance-bypass coverage for Requester Pays. Shortening a GOVERNANCE retention as a non-owner now has focused coverage for the double gate: valid payer without `s3:BypassGovernanceRetention` is denied and does not mutate retention; bypass permission without a valid payer is denied and uncharged; bypass permission plus valid signed payer succeeds and is charged.
- Preserved expected-owner/no-mutation coverage for object retention and legal hold. Wrong `x-amz-expected-bucket-owner` still fails before metadata mutation or XML output, and the tests verify the prior object-lock metadata remains unchanged after failed guards.
- Production `Maxio.S3.Handlers.ObjectLock` did not need code changes in this slice. Review confirmed the existing object-level order is bucket/object request normalization, bucket existence, `ExpectedBucketOwner.verify!`, `RequestPayer.require!`, object-lock bucket state/body parsing, then metadata mutation/output; response charging continues through shared `RequestPayer.put_charged_header/2`.
- Official AWS S3 documentation was checked for this slice: GetObjectRetention, PutObjectRetention, GetObjectLegalHold, PutObjectLegalHold, and Requester Pays acknowledgement/charged-header behavior. The implementation relies on existing shared helpers: `ExpectedBucketOwner`, `RequestPayer`, Object Lock metadata APIs, and Authz governance-bypass evaluation.
- Verification evidence after follow-up fixes: focused `mix test test/maxio/s3/object_lock_e2e_test.exs --max-failures 1` -> 22 tests, 0 failures; adjacent `mix test test/maxio/s3/object_lock_e2e_test.exs test/maxio/s3/request_payment_e2e_test.exs test/maxio/s3/expected_bucket_owner_e2e_test.exs test/maxio/s3/authz_matrix_test.exs --max-failures 1` -> 72 tests, 0 failures; `mix compile --warnings-as-errors` passed; targeted `mix format --check-formatted test/maxio/s3/object_lock_e2e_test.exs docs/superpowers/TODO.md docs/superpowers/STATUS.md` passed; scoped `git diff --check` passed with only the known `STATUS.md` CRLF warning; untracked `docs/superpowers/TODO.md` no-index whitespace check passed.

The implementation lane added the missing payer variants and fixed TODO. Follow-up Codex review returned `APPROVED - no High/Medium/Low findings`.

## Latest Batch Update (2026-07-05 P2 Compat Longtail GetObjectAttributes Requester Pays / Owner Guard closeout)

- Closed scoped slice `P2-COMPAT-LONGTAIL-C` for GetObjectAttributes Requester Pays, expected-bucket-owner, and conditional 304 hardening. This slice intentionally did not run real aws-cli/mc/boto3 external smoke, did not add new ObjectAttributes fields or checksum algorithms, did not implement directory bucket behavior, did not touch production multi-node or release documentation, and deferred full `mix precommit` to a larger compat milestone.
- Fixed `Maxio.S3.Handlers.Object.get_attributes/1` so conditional `304 Not Modified` responses reuse the shared `send_not_modified/3` path. GetObjectAttributes now preserves `ETag`, `Last-Modified`, `x-amz-version-id`, and `x-amz-request-charged` on 304 responses instead of returning a bare 304.
- Added GetObjectAttributes E2E coverage in `test/maxio/s3/object_attributes_test.exs` for a non-owner IAM principal with `s3:GetObjectAttributes` plus `s3:GetObject` on a Requester Pays bucket. Missing payer is denied as structured XML before attributes XML; signed `x-amz-request-payer=requester` succeeds and returns `x-amz-request-charged: requester`; query `x-amz-request-payer=requester` also succeeds and is charged; invalid signed payer and an unsigned payer header are denied as XML errors.
- Added owner-bypass and expected-owner coverage. The bucket owner can call GetObjectAttributes on a Requester Pays bucket without payer acknowledgement and is not charged. A wrong `x-amz-expected-bucket-owner` returns a 403 structured XML error before attributes XML; the correct expected owner succeeds.
- Added a versioned conditional 304 regression for GetObjectAttributes. A non-owner requester with version permissions, signed payer acknowledgement, `versionId`, and matching `If-None-Match` receives `304` with empty body plus `x-amz-request-charged`, `x-amz-version-id`, `ETag`, and `Last-Modified`.
- Official AWS S3 documentation was checked for this slice: GetObjectAttributes `x-amz-request-payer`, `x-amz-expected-bucket-owner`, required object attributes header, version/non-version permissions, response charged header, and conditional request behavior; Requester Pays acknowledgement and charged-header semantics were also checked. The implementation uses existing shared guards: `RequestPayer`, `ExpectedBucketOwner`, Authz ObjectAttributes version permission logic, and the shared object conditional response helper.
- Verification evidence: focused `mix test test/maxio/s3/object_attributes_test.exs --max-failures 1` -> 18 tests, 0 failures; adjacent `mix test test/maxio/s3/object_attributes_test.exs test/maxio/s3/request_payment_e2e_test.exs test/maxio/s3/expected_bucket_owner_e2e_test.exs test/maxio/s3/authz_matrix_test.exs --max-failures 1` -> 68 tests, 0 failures; `mix compile --warnings-as-errors` passed; targeted `mix format --check-formatted lib/maxio/s3/handlers/object.ex test/maxio/s3/object_attributes_test.exs docs/superpowers/TODO.md` passed; scoped `git diff --check` passed; untracked scoped `docs/superpowers/TODO.md` was checked with a `git diff --no-index --check` wrapper and reported no whitespace errors.

## Latest Batch Update (2026-07-05 P2 Local Stress/Fault Campaign closeout)

- Closed scoped slice `P2-STRESS-A` for a local, repeatable stress and fault-injection evidence campaign. This slice does not close real external client compatibility, does not require aws-cli/mc/boto3, does not prove production multi-node/network/disk/PG fault behavior, and does not implement release/deploy or production HSM/KMS readiness. It establishes a local-safe campaign artifact that can be run repeatedly and scaled only through explicit options or environment variables.
- Extended `Maxio.Compat.StreamingStressConfig` with local-safe concurrency controls: `MAXIO_STREAMING_STRESS_CONCURRENCY` and `MAXIO_STREAMING_STRESS_MULTIPART_UPLOADS`. Artifact evidence now records object size, repetitions, chunk sizes, failure attempts, concurrency, multipart upload count, and scenario names.
- Hardened `test/maxio/s3/streaming_stress_matrix_test.exs` from two sequential stress cases to three stress cases. The new case runs concurrent aws-chunked PutObject writes and concurrent multipart aws-chunked uploads through the real S3 endpoint path. Each multipart worker performs a BadDigest failed part attempt, verifies that failed part 2 is not listed, retries with a valid checksum trailer, completes the upload, and verifies the final object body by SHA256.
- Extended `Maxio.Compat.Matrix` with two new optional suites: `storage_fault_matrix` for storage atomic commit, erasure bitrot/heal, and scanner evidence; and `ops_resilience_matrix` for M8 integration, scheduler cadence, and replacement/rebalance workflow evidence. Optional stress/fault suites remain opt-in for the matrix and are used by the stress campaign.
- Added `Maxio.Compat.StressCampaign` and `mix maxio.stress.campaign`. The default campaign skips external probes, runs `streaming_stress_matrix`, `storage_fault_matrix`, and `ops_resilience_matrix`, writes a single `stress_campaign` JSON artifact, records child suite status/counts/commands/evidence, and supports `--fail-on-blocked`, `--fail-on-attention`, `--strict`, `--suite`, `--artifact`, `--json`, and `--mix-cmd`.
- Closed a wrapper false-green risk during implementation. `Maxio.Compat.Matrix` now treats child suites that exit 0 but parse as zero tests or missing test counts as `blocked` with reason `compat_suite_no_tests`; `--fail-on-blocked` then makes both the matrix and the stress campaign exit nonzero. Dedicated matrix and campaign regressions cover this.
- Independent planning and review used separate local Codex read-only lanes, not Claude. Planning returned `APPROVED PLAN: P2-STRESS-A`. Review returned `APPROVED` with no High/Medium findings and one Low finding that `TODO.md` was stale; the TODO was updated. The reviewer also reran focused campaign/config/matrix/task tests and the streaming stress matrix successfully.
- Verification evidence: focused `mix test test/maxio/compat/streaming_stress_config_test.exs test/maxio/compat/matrix_test.exs test/maxio/compat/stress_campaign_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_stress_campaign_test.exs --max-failures 1` -> 28 tests, 0 failures; `mix test test/maxio/s3/streaming_stress_matrix_test.exs --max-failures 1` -> 3 tests, 0 failures; actual local campaign `mix maxio.stress.campaign --fail-on-blocked --artifact artifacts/p2_stress_local.json` -> `gate_status=ok`, streaming stress 3 tests, storage fault 53 tests, ops resilience 8 tests, all 0 failures; adjacent compat Console `mix test test/maxio/ops/compat_matrix/artifacts_test.exs test/maxio/ops/compat_matrix_console_test.exs test/maxio_web/live/ops_compat_live_test.exs --max-failures 1` -> 12 tests, 0 failures; `mix compile --warnings-as-errors` passed; `mix format --check-formatted` passed; scoped `git diff --check` passed with only the known `STATUS.md` CRLF warning. Full closeout verification: `mix precommit` with the low-scheduler Windows configuration completed with 3386 tests, 0 failures, 5 excluded.
- Current completion reading: this materially improves the production-readiness evidence loop, but the whole MinIO clone remains incomplete until real external client compatibility, production multi-node and fault-injection evidence, release/deploy/runbook closure, external KMS/HSM, and long-tail S3/MinIO behavior are proven.

## Latest Batch Update (2026-07-05 P1 IAM Governance and authorization hardening closeout)

- Closed coherent batch `P1-IAM-GOV-A` for IAM governance, local compatibility, and authorization hardening. This batch did not require real aws-cli/mc/boto3 external smoke and did not claim production HSM/KMS readiness; it focused on local IAM Query API surface audit, S3 authorization decision evidence, cross-account policy conditions, access-key lifecycle audit metadata, local-vs-external KMS boundary reporting, and high-risk object-level authorization bypass paths.
- Query API audit found 118 supported IAM Query actions and no locally implemented facade/model action missing from the Query API test surface. The batch therefore shifted from adding action handlers to tightening authorization semantics and observability.
- Hardened S3 Authz with structured allow/deny decision logs carrying request/principal/action/resource/decision/policy source/account metadata, and added `aws:PrincipalAccount` request context support. Local empty-account principals normalize to `local`; anonymous principals normalize to `anonymous`; account-bearing ARNs preserve their account id for policy conditions.
- Corrected owner/root explicit-deny behavior. Explicit Deny now wins for ordinary data actions even when the requester is the bucket owner/root; root recovery special-casing is limited to bucket policy management actions (`GetBucketPolicy`, `PutBucketPolicy`, `DeleteBucketPolicy`) so an owner can recover from a bad bucket policy without bypassing ordinary data Deny statements.
- Added `Maxio.S3.Authz.object_action_allowed?/7` as the single object-level authorization helper for bypass-style checks. `DeleteObjects` per-key authorization and Object Lock governance bypass paths now reuse the same evaluator path as normal S3 requests, including permissions boundary, session policy, resource policy, owner, and explicit-deny semantics. This fixed the independent review findings where those paths had been calling raw policy evaluators and could skip permissions boundaries.
- Hardened IAM Query error audit logging with safe caller and target metadata for access-key lifecycle failures, including root last-active-key protection and cross-`UserName` scope failures, without logging secrets or raw query parameters.
- Added `Maxio.KMS.status/0` non-secret provider boundary reporting. The default local provider is explicitly reported as `:local_compat`, not external KMS/HSM; external production HSM readiness must be declared by a provider through `production_hsm?/0`. Status exposes provider/default key/configured local-key counts only, not master keys or DEKs.
- Independent planning and review used separate local Codex read-only lanes, not Claude. The first review returned `NOT APPROVED` with two High findings: `DeleteObjects` per-key authorization and Object Lock governance bypass could skip permissions boundaries. Both were fixed with the shared object-level Authz helper and regression tests. Follow-up Codex review returned `APPROVED` with no High/Medium/Low findings; the reviewer approved against read-only source inspection and the supplied verification evidence.
- Verification evidence: Query API surface audit `supported_actions=118`, tested action strings `119`, missing `0`; focused/adjacent IAM/Authz/KMS/S3 suites all passed, including `authz_matrix_test`, `iam/query_api_test`, `sse_test`, root explicit-deny focused tests, DeleteObjects/ObjectLock bypass regressions, adjacent identity/copy/delete/MFA/PAB coverage, `mix compile --warnings-as-errors`, `mix format --check-formatted`, scoped static grep and `git diff --check`. Full closeout verification: `mix precommit` with the low-scheduler Windows configuration completed with 3375 tests, 0 failures, 5 excluded.
- Current completion reading: Console/Ops and IAM/Authz governance are now significantly deeper, but the whole MinIO clone is still not complete. Remaining high-value work is real external client compatibility evidence, pressure and fault-injection campaigns, true multi-node/distributed long runs, release/deploy/runbook closure, production external KMS/HSM, and low-frequency S3/MinIO compatibility edges.

## Latest Batch Update (2026-07-05 P1 Console Ops Recovery and DR readiness console closure)

- Closed scoped slice `P1-CONSOLE-OPS-OPS4Q` for `/ops/recovery` read-only recovery and disaster-recovery readiness evidence. This slice adds a dedicated Console surface for operators to inspect P0 live rehearsal bundles, ops gate artifacts, cluster/topology drift, scanner and drive-health evidence, replacement automation, rebalance workflow, upgrade/rollback readiness, and observability readiness without running recovery, fault injection, scheduler, gate, scanner, heal, rebalance, replacement, topology activation, HTTP scrape, System/Mix command, or Repo-write paths.
- Added `Maxio.Ops.RecoveryReadinessConsole` as the read-only projection layer. It reads recent `Maxio.Ops.RehearsalBundle.Artifacts`, recent `Maxio.Ops.Gate.Artifacts`, and the existing cluster/scan/replacement/rebalance/upgrade/observability Console projections through injectable reader functions. Missing artifacts become attention evidence; reader crashes, malformed reader output, malformed artifacts, missing/invalid/future `generated_at`, and unknown readiness shapes fail closed as visible blocked or attention evidence rather than silently healthy output.
- Hardened artifact readiness after independent review. Gate artifacts now distinguish parse/read success from readiness by rolling up `gate_status`, artifact blockers, artifact warnings, and per-check status/blockers/warnings. Rehearsal bundle artifacts now roll up `gate_status`, child result status/gate status, boundary warnings, and `replacement_rehearsal_ready?`. The tests cover ready aggregate evidence, missing/future/stale timestamp handling, malformed and crashing readers, redaction of sensitive reader errors, route/sidebar mounting, required DOM panels, seeded artifact rendering, cross-links, absence of buttons/forms, and the regression where parse-success gate/bundle artifacts still carry failed readiness.

Empty runtime/persisted topology, missing peer/device pressure cache, and missing lock/cache processes are explicit evidence states rather than silently healthy.
- Hardened the projection after independent review. Runtime topology snapshots with missing status now fail closed as blocked evidence instead of hash-matching an empty persisted topology on `"-"`. Top-level and summary rollups now use the same status source and include runtime, persisted, drift, node, pressure, lock, and cache invalidation evidence. Persisted topology hash fallback now returns a blocked section if fingerprint derivation fails, preserving the fail-closed contract even for malformed injected topology evidence.

No buttons, forms, `handle_event` callbacks, JS hooks, `System.cmd`, `Mix.Task`, topology install/activation, lock acquire/force-unlock, pressure refresh, drive sampling, scan, heal, rebalance, or scheduler execution entrypoint was added.

Second, the top-level rollup mapped all-empty evidence to `ok`; this was fixed so all-empty is `empty` and mixed empty+ok is `attention`. Dedicated regressions now cover both cases.

The panel does not add Mix actions, scan/heal/rebalance/decommission controls, or any other operator execution path.

## Previous Batch Update (2026-07-05 P1 Console Ops EvidenceMatrix adversarial schema hardening closure)

- Closed scoped slice `P1-CONSOLE-OPS-OPS4E` for EvidenceMatrix adversarial artifact schema hardening. This slice closes the remaining OPS4A residual where rehearsal artifacts could rely too heavily on an explicit `evidence_kind` field and be counted as P0/M8 evidence even when their JSON structure was spoofed, malformed, or ambiguous.
- Updated `Maxio.Ops.EvidenceMatrix` so artifact kind inference is shape-first and fail-closed. Legacy artifacts without `evidence_kind` can still be inferred from exactly one valid structural shape. Artifacts with explicit `evidence_kind` must use a supported string kind and the shape must match that kind. Non-string explicit kinds, unknown kinds, missing required shape fields, kind/shape mismatches, and multi-shape hybrid artifacts now produce stable error checks instead of covering required rehearsal kinds.
- Preserved the read-only operator evidence boundary. The EvidenceMatrix path still only performs `File.lstat/1`, bounded size checks, file reads, and JSON decoding of already-written artifacts. It does not execute scan, heal, rebalance, replacement, scheduler, decommission, or other operator actions.
- Extended `test/maxio/ops/evidence_matrix_test.exs` as a dedicated regression surface. The tests now cover valid replacement, scheduler, and ops-gate shapes; scheduler-vs-replacement spoofing in both directions; non-string explicit `evidence_kind`; unknown explicit kind with otherwise valid shape; missing scheduler shape fields; invalid ops-gate `checks`; explicit and implicit ambiguous multi-shape artifacts; and adversarial scalar fields that should not crash accepted shapes. Extended `test/maxio/ops/gate_test.exs` so `p0_ops_1` rejects spoofed and ambiguous artifacts without counting them as covered required evidence.
- Independent planning and review used separate local Codex read-only lanes, not Claude. Planning selected `EvidenceMatrix adversarial artifact schema hardening`. Initial review returned `NOT APPROVED` with one High finding for non-string `evidence_kind` falling through to shape inference and one Medium finding for ambiguous multi-shape artifacts being accepted by explicit kind. Both were fixed with regressions. Follow-up Codex review returned `APPROVED` with no High/Medium/Low findings; its remaining coverage note for ambiguous artifacts without explicit `evidence_kind` was closed with an additional direct regression.
- Verification: final focused `mix test test/maxio/ops/evidence_matrix_test.exs test/maxio/ops/gate_test.exs --max-failures 1` -> 24 tests, 0 failures; final adjacent `mix test test/maxio/ops/evidence_matrix_test.exs test/maxio/ops/gate_test.exs test/maxio/ops/live_rehearsal_bundle_test.exs test/maxio/ops/gate/artifacts_test.exs test/maxio_web/live/ops_gate_live_test.exs --max-failures 1` -> 55 tests, 0 failures; `mix format --check-formatted`; `mix compile --warnings-as-errors`; scoped `git diff --check` with only the known `STATUS.md` CRLF warning. ## Previous Batch Update (2026-07-05 P1 Console Ops Gate artifact candidate-window hardening closure)

- Closed scoped slice `P1-CONSOLE-OPS-OPS4D` for Ops Gate artifact candidate-window hardening. This slice closes the OPS4A residual where `max_candidates` was applied before `lstat`/regular-file filtering, letting matching directories, symlinks, or stat-failure pseudo-candidates hide later valid artifacts from `/ops/gate`.
- Updated `Maxio.Ops.Gate.Artifacts` discovery so candidate names remain single-directory, safe-glob filtered, deterministic, and non-recursive, but `max_candidates` now applies to confirmed regular artifact candidates. The scanner uses `File.lstat/2` and increments the candidate window only when a matching entry is under the artifact directory and is a regular file. Directories, symlinks, stat failures, and path races return `nil` and do not consume the regular candidate window.
- Preserved existing invalid-artifact visibility. Oversized regular artifacts, invalid JSON, read errors, and non-ops-gate JSON still flow through `read_artifact/2` and normalize into visible invalid artifact rows instead of being silently ignored. `limit` remains the final returned-artifact limit, while `max_candidates` bounds read work for regular candidates.
- Extended `test/maxio/ops/gate/artifacts_test.exs` so the candidate-window contract covers bounded regular artifact reads, matching directories that sort ahead of valid artifacts, and matching symlinks that sort ahead of valid artifacts when symlink creation is supported on the host. Existing invalid JSON, non-ops JSON, unsafe glob, nested artifact, symlink, oversized artifact, and missing-directory tests continue to guard the prior behavior.
- Independent planning and review used separate local Codex read-only lanes, not Claude. Planning selected `Ops Gate artifact candidate-window hardening` as the highest-value next Console/Ops slice. Initial review returned `APPROVED` with no High/Medium/Low findings. After a local cleanup and `reduce_while` optimization, follow-up Codex review again returned `APPROVED` with no High/Medium/Low findings. Residual risk: if many pseudo-candidates sort before the first regular artifact, those entries still require `lstat` scans; this is the intended tradeoff for the rule that pseudo-candidates must not consume the regular candidate window.
- Verification: final focused `mix test test/maxio/ops/gate/artifacts_test.exs --max-failures 1` -> 11 tests, 0 failures; final adjacent `mix test test/maxio/ops/gate/artifacts_test.exs test/maxio_web/live/ops_gate_live_test.exs test/maxio/ops/gate_test.exs test/maxio/ops/live_rehearsal_bundle_test.exs --max-failures 1` -> 43 tests, 0 failures; `mix format --check-formatted`; `mix compile --warnings-as-errors`; scoped `git diff --check` with only the known `STATUS.md` CRLF warning. ## Previous Batch Update (2026-07-05 P1 S3 GET/HEAD data-plane cross matrix closure)

- Closed `P1-S3-CROSS-01A`, the first GET/HEAD data-plane cross-matrix slice. The batch focused on Requester Pays, Range, conditional headers, explicit `versionId`, latest delete marker behavior, and response header preservation for object reads.
- Tightened `Maxio.S3.Handlers.Object` so GET/HEAD conditional `304 Not Modified` responses preserve object identity and billing evidence: `etag`, `last-modified`, `x-amz-version-id` when present, and `x-amz-request-charged` when the requester-pays charge applies.
- Tightened HEAD object range semantics. HEAD now parses `Range`, returns `416` for unsatisfiable ranges, and for satisfiable ranges returns `200` with an adjusted `Content-Length` while keeping the response body empty. This preserves GET's ranged-body behavior without leaking a body through HEAD.
- Added a cross-matrix requester-pays regression in `test/maxio/s3/request_payment_e2e_test.exs` covering a non-owner principal with `s3:GetObject`, a versioned Requester Pays bucket, explicit old-version reads, latest delete marker GET and HEAD, ranged GET/HEAD with `If-Match`, conditional `304` GET/HEAD with `If-None-Match`, and invalid-range HEAD behavior.
- Official AWS S3 documentation was checked for this batch: GetObject/HeadObject conditional request results, single-range GET behavior, HEAD range content-length semantics, Requester Pays request/response headers, and versioning/delete marker response headers.
- Independent planning and code review used separate local Codex read-only lanes, not Claude. Initial Codex review returned `NOT APPROVED` with 2 Low findings: the new `304` tests did not assert `last-modified`, and latest delete marker coverage included GET but not HEAD. Both gaps were fixed, focused and adjacent verification was rerun, and follow-up Codex review returned `APPROVED` with no High/Medium/Low findings.

## Previous Batch Update (2026-07-05 P1 S3 strict XML restore/select parser closure)

- Added `docs/superpowers/TODO.md` as the live task board for current-batch tracking. `STATUS.md` remains the single source of truth for completed facts, while the TODO board now carries current batch steps, milestone status, follow-up queue, risks, and the real-time update protocol.
- Reworked `Maxio.S3.RequestParsers.SelectObjectContent` onto the shared `XMLTree` parser and made SelectObjectContent XML fail closed for wrong root/wrapping, unknown elements, duplicate singleton fields, empty required fields, nested leaf nodes, mixed non-whitespace text, invalid enum values, invalid booleans, invalid `ScanRange` bounds, multiple input/output serialization formats, and Parquet output. The internal request shape remains `expression`, `expression_type`, `input`, `output`, `request_progress`, `scan_range?`, and `scan_range`.
- Extracted strict `SelectParameters` parsing for RestoreObject use. Restore `SelectParameters` now shares the same Expression/ExpressionType/InputSerialization/OutputSerialization strictness, while RestoreObject keeps `RequestProgress` and `ScanRange` invalid in `SelectParameters` because those are SelectObjectContent request fields rather than RestoreObject `SelectParameters` fields.
- Tightened RestoreObject parser semantics: empty `GlacierJobParameters` now fails closed; duplicate `Tier`, duplicate `Description`, duplicate `GlacierJobParameters/Tier`, duplicate SelectParameters serialization fields, and invalid select serialization enums are rejected. Regular restore accepts retrieval tier only via `GlacierJobParameters/Tier`; Select restore preserves top-level `Tier` instead of silently dropping it. Optional `Description` is parsed through to the request map.
- Added parser regressions for SelectObjectContent Parquet input versus Parquet output, wrong roots/wrappers, unknown nodes, duplicate fields, invalid enums, nested leaves, mixed text, scan range errors, JSON delimiter preservation, and RestoreObject regular/select restore mutual-exclusion edges including select top-level `Tier` preservation.

## Previous Batch Update (2026-07-05 P0 replacement/workflow rehearsal-ready pass evidence)

Default production commands still report disabled automation as blockers instead of silently passing.

Default gate behavior still shells out to production-readiness checks.

## Previous Batch Update (2026-07-04 P0 scheduler-enabled live rehearsal evidence)

- Added `Maxio.Cluster.SchedulerEnabledRehearsal` as a bounded local-safe harness that starts real fresh-drive-heal and rebalance scheduler GenServers, triggers scheduler-controlled `:scheduled` passes, records scheduler ledger rows, forces singleton-lock loser evidence, and writes a `scheduler_live_rehearsal` artifact. The default fixture runners are controlled no-ops, so the rehearsal proves scheduler/lock/ledger behavior without running destructive heal, rebalance, topology mutation, or physical removal.

The CLI supports `--scheduler fresh_drive_heal|rebalance|all`, `--nodes`, expected cadence thresholds, `--require-lock-loser`, `--require-rollups`, `--artifact`, and `--json`. `--require-rollups` now uses the most recent closed window so rollup evidence can be generated immediately without violating the ledger's open-window guard.
- Added `run_scheduled_now/2` to both scheduler modules. This preserves the same scheduled trigger path used by timer ticks while giving tests and bounded operator workflows a synchronous, mailbox-race-free way to exercise scheduler lock, backoff, and ledger behavior. Existing flaky `send(pid, :run) + :sys.get_state(pid)` scheduler tests were moved to this explicit scheduled trigger helper.

- Real bundle/gate probe: `mix run --no-start scripts/m8_p0_ops_live_rehearsal_bundle.exs -- --artifact-dir tmp --run-id p0_ops_3_enabled_bundle_probe --scheduler-enabled --scheduler all --expected-nodes "node_a,node_b" --require-lock-loser --require-rollups --json` returned `LASTEXIT=0`. ## Previous Batch Update (2026-07-04 P0 ops live rehearsal artifact bundle)

- Added `Maxio.Ops.LiveRehearsalBundle` as the local-safe closure driver for the P0 ops evidence matrix. It runs replacement live rehearsal, scheduler live rehearsal, then `Maxio.Ops.Gate --ops-profile p0_ops_1`, and writes a top-level `p0_ops_live_rehearsal_bundle` artifact that links all child artifacts and command results.
- Added `scripts/m8_p0_ops_live_rehearsal_bundle.exs` and `mix maxio.ops.live_rehearsal_bundle` for runbook/CI use. ## Previous Batch Update (2026-07-04 External SDK/CLI deep compatibility smoke matrix)

- Extended `Maxio.Compat.ExternalSmoke` from minimal external CRUD smoke into a deep opt-in external client matrix. The external checks still require an already-running endpoint and configured credentials, but now exercise bucket lifecycle, object write/read, metadata verification, ranged reads, server-side copy, and cleanup through aws-cli, MinIO `mc`, and python-boto3.
- External smoke artifacts now carry step-level `scenario` and `assertion` fields plus per-client `scenario_counts`. `Maxio.Compat.Matrix` preserves those fields in JSON artifacts and prints the scenario summary in stdout, so archived opt-in runs explain what compatibility surface was actually exercised rather than only reporting pass/fail.
- The aws-cli flow now covers `create-bucket`, `head-bucket`, `put-object --metadata maxio-smoke=deep-matrix`, `head-object --query Metadata."maxio-smoke" --output text`, full `get-object`, ranged `get-object --range bytes=0-5`, `copy-object`, copied-object metadata verification, copied-object readback, and cleanup. The quoted JMESPath identifier is required for the hyphenated metadata key.
- The MinIO `mc` flow now covers `mb`, `cp --attr maxio-smoke=deep-matrix`, `stat` metadata evidence, `cat` body verification, object-to-object `cp`, copied-object `stat` metadata evidence, copied-object `cat`, and cleanup. Metadata assertions now verify the command output contains both the metadata key and the expected value.
- The python-boto3 flow now performs an explicit dependency probe before running the deep script. Missing boto3 is reported as `attention` with `external_smoke_client_not_configured` rather than silently skipping. When boto3 is present, the deep script verifies bucket creation, metadata via `head_object`, head range content length, full/ranged `get_object`, `copy_object`, copied body readback, and cleanup.
- The batch was checked against official/client documentation for AWS CLI S3 API `put-object`, `head-object`, `get-object --range`, and `copy-object`; boto3 `head_object`, `get_object`, and `copy_object`; MinIO `mc cp`, `mc cat`, and `mc stat`; and JMESPath quoted identifiers for hyphenated metadata keys. Core S3 handler/storage architecture was not rewritten.
- Independent planning and code review used separate local Codex read-only lanes, not Claude. Planning selected `P0-COMPAT-2B`. First review found 1 Medium issue: AWS/mc metadata assertions were not proving metadata content. That was fixed with real metadata output checks and regressions. Follow-up review found 1 Medium issue: the AWS CLI JMESPath metadata key with a hyphen needed quoting. That was fixed as `Metadata."maxio-smoke"` with a regression. Final Codex follow-up returned `APPROVED` with no High/Medium/Low findings.
- Verification: scoped `mix format --check-formatted lib/maxio/compat/external_smoke.ex test/maxio/compat/external_smoke_test.exs`; focused `mix test test/maxio/compat/external_smoke_test.exs test/maxio/compat/matrix_test.exs --max-failures 1` -> 25 tests, 0 failures; adjacent `mix test test/maxio/compat --max-failures 1` -> 28 tests, 0 failures; real opt-in smoke-only probe `mix maxio.compat.matrix --include-external-smoke --no-internal-suites --skip-external-probes --json --artifact tmp/p0_compat_2b_external_smoke_probe_after_jmespath_fix.json` -> `gate_status=attention`, `total=3`, `attention=3`, `blocked=0` because this machine lacks `aws`, `mc`, and boto3; `mix compile --warnings-as-errors`; final `mix precommit` on the available default localhost:5432 Postgres -> 3223 tests, 0 failures (5 excluded).

## Previous Batch Update (2026-07-04 Streaming soak and trailer compatibility matrix)

- Extended `test/maxio/s3/streaming_matrix_test.exs` from 5 to 8 tests as the next default production-readiness slice for SigV4 aws-chunked uploads. The suite now covers repeated PutObject streaming soak, checksum trailers, and multipart UploadPart trailer behavior in the default compatibility matrix rather than only in broader checksum E2E coverage.
- Added repeated streaming soak coverage for six objects with varied chunk sizes, including bodies crossing the normal `BodyReader` read window. Each object verifies decoded-byte ETag, HEAD `content-length`, absence of persisted transfer `content-encoding`, GET byte length, and GET SHA256 equality.
- Added PutObject checksum trailer coverage for both `STREAMING-UNSIGNED-PAYLOAD-TRAILER` and `STREAMING-AWS4-HMAC-SHA256-PAYLOAD-TRAILER`. The suite verifies `x-amz-checksum-sha256` response echo, decoded object body, content length, no persisted `aws-chunked` content encoding, and a bad trailer checksum failing as `BadDigest` without creating an object.
- Added UploadPart checksum trailer coverage for unsigned and signed trailer modes on the same multipart upload. The suite verifies part checksum response echoes, failed part checksum trailer `BadDigest` does not add part 2 to `ListParts`, the upload remains retryable, and a later valid signed trailer part completes into the expected final object.
- Updated the compat matrix harness fixture for `streaming_matrix` to 8 parsed tests. The default suite order remains `authz_matrix`, `m4_matrix`, `multipart_range_matrix`, `list_conditional_matrix`, `sdk_highfreq_matrix`, `streaming_matrix`.
- The batch was checked against AWS S3 documentation for SigV4 trailing headers and S3 checksum upload semantics: trailer uploads require a trailer-aware `x-amz-content-sha256` value and `x-amz-trailer`; trailing headers are sent after the zero-size chunk; S3 validates uploaded checksum values and rejects mismatches with `BadDigest`; UploadPart echoes checksum headers that were provided with the request.
- Independent code review used a separate local Codex read-only lane via `codex exec -m gpt-5.3-codex-spark -s read-only`, not Claude. Review returned `APPROVED` with no High/Medium/Low findings. Non-blocking residual risks: the default suite-order assertion must be updated when future suites are intentionally added, and ListParts checks still use simple XML substring assertions that can be hardened in a later XML-parsing cleanup batch.

## Previous Batch Update (2026-07-04 S3 compatibility matrix artifact harness)

- Added `Maxio.Compat.Matrix` and `mix maxio.compat.matrix` as the first unified S3 compatibility matrix artifact harness. The default matrix runs the internal self-signed S3 compatibility suites `test/maxio/s3/authz_matrix_test.exs` and `test/maxio/s3/m4_matrix_test.exs` through a stable CLI/reporting layer, with `--suite` filters for narrower runs.
- The artifact schema is versioned and includes `schema_version`, `generated_at`, `run_id`, `git_sha`, `node`, `mix_env`, parent command/config, aggregate counts, top-level `blockers`/`warnings`, internal suite entries, and external client availability entries. The default artifact path is `artifacts/s3_compat_matrix_<timestamp>_<unique>.json`, with `--artifact PATH` override.
- Gate semantics are explicit: `error > blocked > attention > ok`. Internal suite command exceptions and malformed external probe contracts fail closed as `error`. Internal suite non-zero exits become `blocked`. External aws-cli, MinIO `mc`, and python-boto3 availability gaps are `attention` by default; `--fail-on-blocked` and `--fail-on-attention` / `--strict` promote those reporting states into failing exits.
- External client evidence now executes availability probes instead of only checking PATH. `aws` and `mc` run `--version` when present; python-boto3 probes `python`, `python3`, then Windows `py` with an importlib module check. Missing or broken external clients remain reporting-only unless strict/fail flags are requested, and malformed probe statuses produce explicit `external_client_status_invalid` blockers.
- Regressions cover default internal suite selection and artifact fields, reporting-only external attention versus strict failure, malformed external status fail-closed behavior, external probe exceptions, actual external command execution with Windows `py` fallback, internal suite failure gates, command exceptions, suite filtering, invalid suite usage, and duplicate suite filters running each selected suite once in stable default order.

First code review found 2 Medium and 2 Low issues around external status fail-closed behavior, PATH-only CLI probes, Windows `py` coverage, and missing edge tests; all were fixed. Follow-up review returned `APPROVED` with no High/Medium/Low findings.

## Previous Batch Update (2026-07-04 M8 ops gate artifact discovery hardening)

- Hardened `Maxio.Ops.Gate.Artifacts` discovery for the Console ops gate artifact page. The reader no longer uses `Path.wildcard/1`; it now performs a single-directory `File.ls/1`, applies a safe filename-glob matcher, sorts candidate names deterministically, and applies `max_candidates` before `File.lstat/2` or artifact reads. This keeps the Console path read-only while reducing pre-limit stat/read work in large artifact directories.
- The artifact glob and candidate names now fail closed when they contain path separators or `..`, so configured globs cannot escape the artifact directory and traversal-looking artifact names are ignored. Discovery remains non-recursive by design: nested artifacts are not read through the Console listing.
- Path containment is now direct-child based after `Path.expand/1` normalization, which fixes the root-directory false-negative found by independent review while preserving the regular-file-only lstat boundary. Matching directories and supported-platform symlinks are not read as artifacts; oversized artifacts, invalid JSON, and non-ops-gate JSON still degrade into visible invalid artifact rows instead of crashing.
- Regressions now cover newest-first normalization, invalid JSON visibility, non-ops-gate JSON visibility, candidate limiting before lstat/read, unsafe glob escape attempts, nested artifacts, `..` file names, matching directories, symlink lstat behavior when the platform supports symlinks, oversized artifacts, and missing directories.
- Independent planning and code review used separate local Codex read-only lanes via `codex exec -m gpt-5.3-codex-spark -s read-only`, not Claude. Planning returned `GO` for `M8P9-OPS-GATE-RESIDUAL-1`. Code review found one Low root-directory containment false-negative, which was fixed; follow-up review returned `APPROVED` with no High/Medium/Low findings.

## Previous Batch Update (2026-07-04 M8 unified ops gate artifact harness)

- Added `Maxio.Ops.Gate`, `mix maxio.ops.gate`, and `scripts/m8_ops_gate.exs` as a unified M8 operator gate/artifact harness. The harness shells out to the existing operator-facing JSON gate tasks, archives every child report, and computes one release/preflight `gate_status`.
- Default checks are deliberately read-only/no-start oriented: `maxio.scheduler.status --json`, `maxio.replacement.automation_status --json`, `maxio.replacement.workflow --json`, and `maxio.rebalance.workflow --json`. Heavyweight or potentially mutating checks are explicit opt-in only: `--include-scanner` runs `maxio.reconcile_index --dry-run --json`; `--include-fresh-drive-heal` runs `maxio.heal.fresh_drives --dry-run --json` unless `--allow-mutating` is explicit; `--include-foreground-scan` requires `--allow-mutating` before running `maxio.scan --json`; `--include-scheduler-evidence` maps the scheduler evidence window/node options into `maxio.scheduler.evidence --json`.
- The artifact schema is versioned and includes `schema_version`, `generated_at`, `run_id`, `git_sha`, `node`, `mix_env`, parent command/config, aggregate counts, top-level `blockers`/`warnings`, and each child check's id/task/command/mode/required/status/exit status/duration/parsed output/raw output. The default artifact path is `artifacts/m8_ops_gate_<timestamp>_<unique>.json`, with `--artifact PATH` override.
- Aggregate gate semantics are explicit: `error > blocked > attention > ok`. Child JSON parse failures, command exceptions, and unexplained non-zero exits become `status=error` and fail closed even without fail flags. `blocked` and `attention` remain reporting-only by default; `--fail-on-blocked` fails blocked/error gates, and `--fail-on-attention` / `--strict` also fail attention gates. Text output summarizes every check and top-level blocker/warning; `--json` emits the full artifact to stdout.
- A real default probe `mix maxio.ops.gate --json --artifact tmp/m8_ops_gate_probe.json` executed end-to-end and wrote the artifact. On the local dev config the aggregate gate reported `blocked` because replacement automation schedulers are disabled; the command still exited 0 because fail flags were not requested, proving the intended reporting-only default behavior.
- Regressions cover default command selection with no scanner/scan/heal side effects, reporting-only attention versus `--fail-on-attention`, fail-closed unparseable child JSON, opt-in scanner and fresh-drive dry-run command construction, foreground scan requiring `--allow-mutating`, scheduler evidence option mapping, artifact fields/counts, and JSON stdout behavior.
- Independent review used a separate local Codex read-only lane via `codex exec -m gpt-5.3-codex-spark -s read-only`, not Claude. The review returned `APPROVED — no High/Medium/Low findings`.
- Verification: focused `mix test test/maxio/ops/gate_test.exs --max-failures 1` -> 6 tests, 0 failures; adjacent gate/rehearsal/task suite -> 58 tests, 0 failures; optional scanner/scan/fresh-drive child task suite -> 29 tests, 0 failures; `mix compile --warnings-as-errors`; scoped `mix format --check-formatted`; scoped `git diff --check`; real default `mix maxio.ops.gate --json --artifact tmp/m8_ops_gate_probe.json` probe -> exit 0 and artifact written; final `mix precommit` on the available default localhost:5432 Postgres -> 3157 tests, 0 failures (5 excluded).

## Previous Batch Update (2026-07-04 M8 replacement/rebalance workflow operator gates)

- `Maxio.Cluster.Replacement.Workflow` now emits stable top-level gate evidence for the replacement runbook while preserving the existing workflow fields. Reports include `gate_status`, gate `blockers`, `warnings`, and the operation-only `workflow_blockers` alongside `status`, `phase`, `automation_profile`, `rebalance_workflow`, and `next_action`.
- Replacement workflow gate semantics are explicit: `gate_status=blocked` is returned for failed/blocked workflow status, operation blockers, or replacement automation profile blockers; `gate_status=attention` is returned for automation warnings or a remaining actionable `next_action` when no blocker is present; otherwise the gate is `ok`. Automation blockers are intentionally promoted into the top-level gate blockers so live rehearsal and CI can fail on incomplete production automation readiness without scraping nested profile fields.
- `mix maxio.replacement.workflow` now supports `--fail-on-attention` while preserving `--fail-on-blocked`. Text output adds `gate_status`, automation warning counts, top-level warnings, and per-blocker/per-warning lines; JSON output keeps the old report shape and adds the new gate fields. Both fail flags emit evidence before `Mix.raise`.
- `Maxio.Cluster.Rebalance.Workflow.summary/2` now carries `gate_status` and `warnings` directly in the read-only workflow report. In-progress workflows with a remaining `next_action` gate as `attention`, destructive next-action phases also warn, blocked workflows stay `blocked`, and complete rebalance closure reports gate `ok`.
- `mix maxio.rebalance.workflow` now supports `--fail-on-blocked` and `--fail-on-attention`, emits gate evidence in text and JSON, treats `not_started` as `gate_status=ok`, and no longer calls `Mix.Task.run("app.start")`. It starts only Repo dependencies (`ecto_sql`, `postgrex`, `Maxio.Repo`) for read-only workflow collection, so `mix run --no-start` cannot accidentally start fresh-drive, rebalance, or lifecycle scheduler processes.
- Regressions cover replacement core gate blockers/warnings/ok/attention paths, replacement CLI JSON/text output and fail-on gates, rebalance core in-progress/blocked/complete gate semantics, rebalance CLI JSON/text output, `--fail-on-blocked`, `--fail-on-attention` for both attention and blocked gates, `not_started` ok gates, and no-start subprocess checks for both replacement and rebalance workflow tasks.
- Independent review used a separate local Codex read-only lane via `codex exec -m gpt-5.3-codex-spark -s read-only`, not Claude. The review returned `APPROVED / no High/Medium/Low findings`; its non-blocking suggestions were reviewed and did not require code changes because the attention-without-next-action regression was already covered and the TODO encoding concern was a Codex PowerShell read artifact, while local UTF-8 reads are correct.
- Verification: focused `mix test test/maxio/cluster/replacement_workflow_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_replacement_workflow_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_workflow_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_rebalance_workflow_test.exs --max-failures 1` -> 33 tests, 0 failures; adjacent live rehearsal/automation/rebalance scheduler/closure suite -> 68 tests, 0 failures; `mix compile --warnings-as-errors`; scoped `mix format --check-formatted`; scoped `git diff --check`; final `mix precommit` on the available default localhost:5432 Postgres -> 3151 tests, 0 failures (5 excluded).

## Previous Batch Update (2026-07-04 M8 scheduler status operator evidence gate)

- `mix maxio.scheduler.status` now emits a stable operator gate surface while preserving the existing state/run/rollup/cadence evidence output. JSON reports now include top-level `gate_status`, `blockers`, and `warnings` alongside the existing `states`, `runs`, `rollups`, and optional `cadence` payloads.
- Text output remains backward-compatible for the existing `[maxio scheduler.status]`, `[maxio scheduler.run]`, `[maxio scheduler.rollup]`, and `[maxio scheduler.cadence]` lines, and now adds `[maxio scheduler.status.summary]` plus per-blocker/per-warning lines so operators can read gate evidence without scraping JSON.
- New gate semantics are explicit and reporting-only by default: `gate_status=blocked` is returned for current failed/blocked scheduler state, state `alert_status=alert`, or requested cadence health that is not `ok`; `gate_status=attention` is returned for recovered recent failed/blocked run evidence, rollup windows containing failed/blocked runs, state warnings/consecutive failures, or cadence windows with scheduled problem counts when no blocker is present.
- `--fail-on-blocked` raises only after emitting evidence for blocked reports. `--fail-on-attention` raises after emitting evidence for both blocked and attention reports. Normal status invocations remain non-raising reporting commands.
- The command is now read-only/no-start for application schedulers: it starts only Repo dependencies (`ecto_sql`, `postgrex`, `Maxio.Repo`) instead of `Mix.Task.run("app.start")`, so `mix run --no-start` status collection cannot accidentally start fresh-drive heal, rebalance, or lifecycle scheduler processes.
- Regressions cover text summary evidence, JSON blocked gate fields, `--fail-on-blocked` evidence-before-raise, `--fail-on-attention` for recovered recent problem evidence, state-only behavior, cadence non-health blockers, cadence text blocker lines, rollup ok gates, invalid flags, and no-start subprocess assertions including scheduler process names and `MAXIO_APP_STARTED=false`.
- Independent review used separate local Codex read-only lanes via `codex exec -m gpt-5.3-codex-spark -s read-only`, not Claude. The first review returned `APPROVED` with no High/Medium/Low findings and noted non-blocking test robustness risks. Follow-up found 2 Low test issues (format-sensitive JSON assertion and Windows temp path spacing risk); both were fixed by parsing the JSON output line and using a repo-local relative `tmp/` script path. Final follow-up returned `APPROVED` with no remaining High/Medium/Low findings.

## Previous Batch Update (2026-07-04 M8 fresh-drive heal operator evidence gate)

- `mix maxio.heal.fresh_drives` now emits a stable machine-readable operator evidence report with `--json`. The JSON surface covers the fresh-drive heal report counters (`drives_considered`, `drives_selected`, `drives_cleared`, waiting/skipped set counts, scanned/healed/missing/skipped/error counters, partial/truncated state), `gate_status`, `blockers`, `warnings`, and optional rebalance enqueue/advance evidence.
- The default text output keeps the existing headline fresh-drive heal counters and now adds `gate_status`, backend/bucket/scope fields, blocker/warning counts, per-reason blocker/warning lines, and scheduler error reason evidence when a bridge failure occurs. Operators can continue reading text while automation consumes JSON without scraping.
- New gate semantics are explicit: `gate_status=blocked` is returned when the heal pass is not a trustworthy closure signal (`waiting`, `partial`, `partial_scope`, `incomplete`), when object-level unrecoverable/missing/skipped/error counters are non-zero, when the opt-in rebalance enqueue/advance bridge fails or blocks, or when the scheduler bridge itself returns `{:error, reason}`. `gate_status=attention` is returned for dry-runs and successful actionable evidence such as cleared markers, healed objects/shards, skipped same-set drives, created rebalance jobs, skipped enqueue, or partial rebalance advance.
- `--fail-on-blocked` raises after emitting evidence only for blocked reports. `--fail-on-attention` raises after emitting evidence for both blocked and attention reports. Both flags are opt-in, so normal operator invocations remain reporting-only.
- Scheduler bridge errors now produce a synthetic blocked evidence report (`status=scheduler_error`, `error_reason`, counters zeroed except `errors=1`) instead of failing before JSON/text output. This closes the gate-safety gap found by independent review and keeps runbooks from losing machine-readable failure evidence.
- Regressions cover dry-run text attention evidence, dry-run JSON evidence, `--fail-on-attention` after dry-run JSON, partial-scope blocked JSON for `--fail-on-blocked`, `--fail-on-attention` also failing blocked reports, throttle flags, rebalance enqueue text and JSON evidence, rebalance advance nested JSON evidence including `phases`/`blockers`, scheduler bridge failure JSON evidence before raising, bounded closure text evidence, and invalid rebalance advance step handling.
- Independent review used separate local Codex read-only lanes via `codex exec -m gpt-5.3-codex-spark -s read-only`, not Claude. The first review found 1 Medium and 2 Low issues: scheduler bridge errors raised before evidence output, rebalance advance JSON lacked coverage, and scheduler failure evidence lacked coverage. These were fixed. Follow-up Codex review returned `APPROVED` with no High/Medium/Low findings.

## Previous Batch Update (2026-07-04 M8 scan CLI operator evidence gate)

- `mix maxio.scan` now emits a stable machine-readable operator evidence report with `--json`. The report covers the existing foreground scrubber summary surface: scan status/backend, bucket/object usage counts, object scan/heal counters, index reconcile pre-pass counters, drive health counters, `gate_status`, `blockers`, and `warnings`.
- The default text output remains backward-compatible for the existing headline counters and now adds `gate_status`, blocker/warning counts, and per-reason blocker/warning lines. Operators can keep using text output while CI/runbooks consume JSON without scraping.
- New gate semantics are explicit: `gate_status=blocked` is returned when the scan itself is not trustworthy (`status=partial`) or the requested index pre-pass is blocked/crashed (`index_authority_mismatch`, `index_no_topology`, `index_error`); `gate_status=attention` is returned when the scan ran but found actionable object, index, orphan-shard, or drive-health evidence; otherwise `gate_status=ok`.
- `--fail-on-blocked` raises after emitting evidence only for blocked reports. `--fail-on-attention` raises after emitting evidence for both blocked and attention reports. Both flags are opt-in, so normal operator invocations remain reporting-only.
- `Maxio.Cluster.Scrubber` now promotes per-bucket scan failures to `status: :partial` instead of silently returning a fully `:ok` summary. Status propagation is handled in `merge_summary/2`, so one failed bucket keeps the whole round visibly partial while successful bucket counts still merge normally. This closes the gate-safety gap found by independent review.
- `Scrubber` now also allows `throttle_now_fun` / `throttle_sleep_fun` to be supplied through `config :maxio, :scrubber`, matching the existing run-time injection model and enabling realistic Mix task regression coverage for bucket scan failure gates without test-only CLI flags.
- Regressions cover normal text output with `gate_status=ok`, JSON ok evidence, authority-mismatch blocked JSON evidence, `--fail-on-blocked`, `--fail-on-attention` on blocked reports, orphan-shard attention JSON evidence, `--fail-on-attention` on attention reports, direct `Scrubber.run_once/1` partial status propagation, and `mix maxio.scan --fail-on-blocked` on a real partial scrubber report.
- Independent review used separate local Codex read-only lanes via `codex exec -m gpt-5.3-codex-spark -s read-only`, not Claude. The first review found 1 Medium and 1 Low issue: bucket scan failures were not promoted into a non-OK scrubber summary, so gates could miss them, and that path lacked coverage. The architecture-level status propagation and regressions were added. Follow-up Codex review returned `APPROVED` with no High/Medium/Low findings.
- Verification: initial focused `mix test test/mix/tasks/maxio_scan_test.exs --max-failures 1` -> 9 tests, 0 failures; post-fix direct impact `mix test test/mix/tasks/maxio_scan_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/scrubber_test.exs --max-failures 1` -> 26 tests, 0 failures; post-fix adjacent `mix test test/mix/tasks/maxio_scan_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/scrubber_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/drive_health_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/drive_identity_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/pressure_aggregator_test.exs test/maxio/storage/scanner_test.exs test/maxio/meta_reconcile_test.exs --max-failures 1` -> 90 tests, 0 failures; `mix compile --warnings-as-errors`; scoped `mix format --check-formatted`; scoped `git diff --check`; final `mix precommit` on the available default localhost:5432 Postgres -> 3132 tests, 0 failures (5 excluded).

## Previous Batch Update (2026-07-04 M8 scanner operator evidence gate)

- `mix maxio.reconcile_index` now emits a stable machine-readable operator evidence report with `--json`. The JSON report contains the existing scanner counters plus `status`, `gate_status`, `blockers`, and `warnings`, so CI/runbooks can distinguish safe no-change runs from blocked runs and drift/repair evidence without scraping text output.
