## Previous Batch Update (2026-07-02 IAM Role Managed Policy Attachment Query API)

- IAM role managed-policy attachment management is now implemented on the IAM Query API path for root operators: `AttachRolePolicy`, `ListAttachedRolePolicies`, and `DetachRolePolicy`. The new actions preserve the existing root-only gate, SigV4 `iam` service-scope requirement, duplicate-parameter rejection, bounded POST form parsing, strict unsupported-parameter rejection, Store-behaviour boundary, and IAM Query XML namespace output.
- Query role attachment uses AWS-shaped `RoleName` input rather than role ARN, validates the same role-name character/length contract as persisted roles, and resolves missing roles or missing managed policy ARNs to `NoSuchEntity`. Invalid `RoleName`, invalid local `PolicyArn`, invalid `PathPrefix`, and unsupported parameters such as `PermissionsBoundary` fail closed with structured IAM Query errors.
- `ListAttachedRolePolicies` reuses the shared attached-policy XML shape (`AttachedPolicies.member.PolicyName` / `PolicyArn`) with `MaxItems` / `Marker` pagination, `PathPrefix` filtering, `IsTruncated`, and next-marker output. Empty attach/detach successes use the standard Query `ResponseMetadata` envelope.
- Managed-policy attachment quotas now cover roles in the IAM core path as well as Query: a role can attach at most 10 managed policies, duplicate attaches remain idempotent success, and over-quota attach returns `LimitExceeded`. Role attach goes through the shared managed-policy quota/lock helper, keeping delete-vs-attach serialization and the Store abstraction aligned with user/group attachment behavior.
- Coverage now exercises GET and POST Query paths for user/group/role attachment, role missing-resource and malformed-parameter failures, role quota overflow, role strict facade list/detach, and the adjacent STS AssumeRole attached-policy authorization path. Independent Codex review reported no findings and `APPROVED`; residual notes are no live AWS probe for detach of an existing but unattached role/policy pair and no MinIO/AWS interoperability run for this Query surface yet.
- Verification: `mix compile --warnings-as-errors`; scoped `mix format --check-formatted`; scoped `git diff --check`; focused `mix test test/maxio/iam_test.exs --max-failures 1` -> 32 tests, 0 failures; focused `mix test test/maxio/iam/query_api_test.exs --max-failures 1` -> 26 tests, 0 failures; adjacent STS `mix test test/maxio/sts/query_api_test.exs --max-failures 1` -> 70 tests, 0 failures; independent Codex reviewer reran the same focused files successfully; final `mix precommit` -> 2775 tests, 0 failures (5 excluded).

## Previous Batch Update (2026-07-02 IAM Managed Policy Version Mutation Query API)

- IAM customer managed-policy version mutation is now implemented on the IAM Query API path for root operators: `CreatePolicyVersion`, `SetDefaultPolicyVersion`, and `DeletePolicyVersion`; existing `GetPolicyVersion` / `ListPolicyVersions` now read the real version table instead of the previous single-`v1` shim. The actions preserve the root-only gate, SigV4 `iam` service-scope requirement, duplicate-parameter rejection, bounded POST form parsing, strict unsupported-parameter rejection, Store-behaviour boundary, and IAM Query XML namespace output.
- Managed policies now have `managed_policy_versions` plus `managed_policies.next_version_number`. `CreatePolicy` / Store create backfill `v1` as default; `CreatePolicyVersion` creates monotonic non-reused `vN` ids, enforces the AWS 5-active-version cap, and optionally makes the new version default with `SetAsDefault=true`. `SetDefaultPolicyVersion` updates both the version table default bit and the `managed_policies.document` mirror used by authorization/evaluator paths. `DeletePolicyVersion` rejects default-version deletion, and `DeletePolicy` now returns `DeleteConflict` while any non-default versions remain.
- The new migration is schema self-documenting per AGENTS: `next_version_number`, `managed_policy_versions` table/columns/indexes, and timestamp columns all have Chinese COMMENTs, with index/column comments cleared in `down`. Existing managed policies are backfilled into `v1` default rows during migration without requiring `pgcrypto`.
- Query XML now follows AWS shape: `CreatePolicyVersion` and `ListPolicyVersions` return `PolicyVersion` metadata without `Document`; `GetPolicyVersion` returns the selected version with URL-encoded `Document`; `GetPolicy` / `ListPolicies` report the live `DefaultVersionId`. Invalid `SetAsDefault`, malformed policy documents, missing versions, default-version delete, version-limit overflow, non-default-version policy delete, malformed ARNs, and unsupported params all fail closed with structured IAM Query errors.
- Independent Codex review first found one P2 issue: `timestamps()` columns in the new migration lacked DB comments. The migration now comments `managed_policy_versions.inserted_at/updated_at` and clears those comments in `down`. Review also noted a residual evaluator-path test gap; Store tests now attach a managed policy to a real role, assert v1 authorization actions, switch default to v2, and assert the attached role sees the v2 action. Follow-up Codex review: `APPROVED`. Remaining residual risk: no explicit concurrent `CreatePolicyVersion` race test yet; the implementation serializes through the managed-policy row `FOR UPDATE`.
- Verification: `mix compile --warnings-as-errors`; `mix format --check-formatted`; scoped `git diff --check`; focused `mix test test/maxio/policy/store_test.exs test/maxio/policy/store_optional_callbacks_test.exs --max-failures 1` -> 16 tests, 0 failures; focused `mix test test/maxio/iam_test.exs --max-failures 1` -> 31 tests, 0 failures; focused `mix test test/maxio/iam/query_api_test.exs --max-failures 1` -> 26 tests, 0 failures; final `mix precommit` -> 2774 tests, 0 failures (5 excluded).

## Previous Batch Update (2026-07-02 IAM Managed Policy Tags / Entities Query API)

- IAM customer managed-policy tagging and entity lookup is now implemented on the IAM Query API path for root operators: `TagPolicy`, `UntagPolicy`, `ListPolicyTags`, and `ListEntitiesForPolicy`. The actions preserve the existing root-only gate, SigV4 `iam` service-scope requirement, duplicate-parameter rejection, bounded POST form parsing, strict unsupported-parameter rejection, Store-behaviour boundary, and IAM Query XML namespace output.
- Managed policy tags remain case-sensitive, matching AWS managed policy tag semantics: `CostCenter` and `costcenter` can coexist, exact duplicate keys fail validation, `TagPolicy` overwrites the same exact key, and `UntagPolicy` removes exact keys while ignoring missing keys. The EctoStore update path now validates the merged tag map before writing, so an existing 49-tag policy cannot accept two new keys and persist 51 tags; regression coverage verifies no partial write occurs.
- `ListPolicyTags` returns sorted `Tags.member.Key/Value` output through the shared tag ordering helper and supports `MaxItems` / `Marker` pagination with `IsTruncated` and next-marker output. Missing policies return `NoSuchEntity`; invalid tag keys, reserved `aws:` prefixes, bad pagination, malformed policy ARNs, and unsupported parameters fail closed with structured IAM Query errors.
- `ListEntitiesForPolicy` now returns AWS-shaped `PolicyGroups`, `PolicyUsers`, and `PolicyRoles` members for local users/groups/roles attached to the managed policy. Entity lookup stays behind `Maxio.Policy.Store.list_managed_session_policy_entities/1`, with EctoStore joining attachments back to live entity rows so orphaned attachments do not leak into the response. It supports `EntityFilter`, `PathPrefix`, `PolicyUsageFilter`, `MaxItems`, and `Marker`; local `LocalManagedPolicy` / `AWSManagedPolicy` filters and non-root path prefixes return empty pages in the current single-account implementation. `PermissionsBoundary` usage is implemented by the latest IAM PermissionsBoundary batch.
- Independent Codex review first found one Low issue: `TagPolicy` validated only incoming tags and not the merged tag total, allowing a 49+2 overflow. The Store update path now validates the merged map before `Repo.update`, and Store + Query regressions cover the no-partial-write behavior. Follow-up Codex review: `APPROVED`.
- Verification: `mix compile --warnings-as-errors`; `mix format --check-formatted`; focused `mix test test/maxio/policy/store_test.exs test/maxio/policy/store_optional_callbacks_test.exs --max-failures 1` -> 14 tests, 0 failures; focused `mix test test/maxio/iam/query_api_test.exs --max-failures 1` -> 26 tests, 0 failures; IAM core `mix test test/maxio/iam_test.exs --max-failures 1` -> 30 tests, 0 failures; scoped `git diff --check` passed; final `mix precommit` -> 2771 tests, 0 failures (5 excluded).

## Previous Batch Update (2026-07-02 IAM Managed Policy Lifecycle Query API)

- IAM customer managed-policy lifecycle is now implemented on the IAM Query API path for root operators: `CreatePolicy`, `GetPolicy`, `GetPolicyVersion`, `ListPolicyVersions`, `ListPolicies`, and `DeletePolicy`. The actions preserve the existing root-only gate, SigV4 `iam` service-scope requirement, duplicate-parameter rejection, bounded POST form parsing, strict unsupported-parameter rejection, and IAM Query XML namespace output.
- Managed policy rows now persist Query-visible metadata: `description` and case-sensitive `tags`, with a new commented migration on `managed_policies`. `CreatePolicy` validates `PolicyName`, managed policy `Path`, `Description`, tag shape, identity-policy document syntax, no explicit `Principal`, AWS document character/length rules, compact managed-policy size quota, account policy quota, and duplicate ARN/name conflicts before storing.
- Query `GetPolicy` / `CreatePolicy` render AWS-shaped `Policy` metadata including stable synthetic `PolicyId`, `Path`, `PolicyName`, `Arn`, `DefaultVersionId=v1`, `AttachmentCount`, `PermissionsBoundaryUsageCount`, `IsAttachable=true`, timestamps, `Description`, and `Tags`. `ListPolicies` renders summary `Policy` entries without tags and supports `Scope`, `OnlyAttached`, `PolicyUsageFilter`, `PathPrefix`, `MaxItems`, and `Marker`; local `Scope=AWS` returns an empty page, while `PolicyUsageFilter=PermissionsBoundary` is implemented by the latest IAM PermissionsBoundary batch.
- Local managed policies remain single-version for now: `GetPolicyVersion` supports AWS `VersionId` syntax but only `v1` exists, returning a URL-encoded policy document; `ListPolicyVersions` returns the `v1` metadata member without the document. Legal but missing versions such as `v2.test` return `NoSuchEntity`.
- `DeletePolicy` is strict for Query: missing policies return `NoSuchEntity`, attached policies return `DeleteConflict`, and successful deletion requires no current user/group/role attachments. IAM attach/detach/delete paths now serialize on a policy-scoped advisory lock, and EctoStore policy deletion locks the managed policy row `FOR UPDATE` before checking attachments and deleting, avoiding FK race exceptions while keeping the older Store facade's idempotent missing-delete behavior.
- Independent Codex review first found two issues: `DeletePolicy` was not atomic with concurrent attach/delete, and `VersionId` rejected AWS-valid dotted version IDs as invalid instead of missing. Both were fixed, with a `v2.test` regression added. Follow-up Codex review: `APPROVED`.
- Verification: `mix compile --warnings-as-errors`; focused `mix test test/maxio/iam/query_api_test.exs --max-failures 1` -> 26 tests, 0 failures; adjacent Store/IAM Query batch `mix test test/maxio/policy/store_test.exs test/maxio/policy/store_optional_callbacks_test.exs test/maxio/iam/query_api_test.exs --max-failures 1` -> 39 tests, 0 failures; IAM core `mix test test/maxio/iam_test.exs --max-failures 1` -> 30 tests, 0 failures; `mix format --check-formatted` and `git diff --check` passed; final `mix precommit` -> 2770 tests, 0 failures (5 excluded).

## Previous Batch Update (2026-07-02 IAM Managed Policy Attachment Query API)

- IAM user/group managed-policy attachment management is now implemented on the IAM Query API path for root operators: `AttachUserPolicy`, `ListAttachedUserPolicies`, `DetachUserPolicy`, `AttachGroupPolicy`, `ListAttachedGroupPolicies`, and `DetachGroupPolicy`. The actions preserve the root-only gate, SigV4 `iam` service-scope requirement, duplicate-parameter rejection, bounded POST form parsing, strict unsupported-parameter rejection, and IAM Query XML namespace output.
- Query attach/list/detach reuses the existing managed-policy registry and user/group attachment store callbacks through new strict `Maxio.IAM` facade functions. Query strictness resolves missing users/groups and missing managed policy ARNs to `NoSuchEntity`, validates local `arn:aws:iam:::policy/...` `PolicyArn` values without trimming, and validates managed-policy `PathPrefix` instead of silently returning an empty page on malformed input.
- `ListAttachedUserPolicies` and `ListAttachedGroupPolicies` render AWS-shaped `AttachedPolicies.member.PolicyName` / `PolicyArn`, support `MaxItems`/`Marker` pagination, `PathPrefix` filtering, `IsTruncated`, and next-marker output. Empty attach/detach successes use standard Query `ResponseMetadata` envelopes.
- Managed-policy attachment quotas are enforced at the IAM core path for both Query and CLI/Console callers: user and group attach operations allow at most 10 attached managed policies, treat duplicate attach as idempotent success, and return `LimitExceeded` HTTP 409 when the quota would be exceeded. The quota check and attach run under the existing per-principal advisory-lock pattern to avoid concurrent over-attach.
- IAM Query unsupported-parameter filtering now permits standard SigV4 presigned `X-Amz-*` query authentication parameters, so presigned IAM Query GET requests are not rejected as unimplemented operation parameters after the SigV4 plug has validated them.
- Independent Codex review first found two Medium issues: presigned IAM Query GETs were rejected by unsupported-parameter filtering, and attach operations lacked managed-policy attachment quotas. Both were fixed, with presigned and quota regressions added. Follow-up Codex review: `APPROVED`.
- Verification: `mix compile --warnings-as-errors`; focused `mix test test/maxio/iam/query_api_test.exs --max-failures 1` -> 23 tests, 0 failures; adjacent IAM/Policy/Authz batch `mix test test/maxio/iam_test.exs test/maxio/policy/store_test.exs test/maxio/policy/store_optional_callbacks_test.exs test/maxio/s3/identity_policy_authz_test.exs test/maxio/iam/query_api_test.exs --max-failures 1` -> 77 tests, 0 failures; `mix format --check-formatted` passed; final `mix precommit` -> 2767 tests, 0 failures (5 excluded).

## Previous Batch Update (2026-07-02 IAM Inline Policy Query API)

- IAM user/group inline policy management is now implemented on the IAM Query API path for root operators: `PutUserPolicy`, `GetUserPolicy`, `ListUserPolicies`, `DeleteUserPolicy`, `PutGroupPolicy`, `GetGroupPolicy`, `ListGroupPolicies`, and `DeleteGroupPolicy`. The actions preserve the existing root-only gate, SigV4 `iam` service-scope requirement, duplicate-parameter rejection, bounded POST form parsing, strict unsupported-parameter rejection, and IAM Query XML namespace output.
- The implementation reuses the existing `principal_policies` table and policy store abstraction. `Maxio.Policy.Store` now has an optional `get_principal_policy/2` callback, with EctoStore support and a `:not_supported` fallback for legacy plugin backends, so Query get/delete can distinguish missing policy rows without changing the core request pipeline or adding a new storage contract.
- Query `Put*Policy` validates `PolicyName`, the 131072-character AWS inline policy document maximum, allowed document character range, no explicit identity-policy `Principal`/`NotPrincipal`, duplicate JSON object keys at any nesting level, and aggregate inline policy quota before replacing the existing row. Invalid replacement fails before storage, preserving the prior policy. `MalformedPolicyDocument` maps to HTTP 400 and inline quota overflow maps to `LimitExceeded` HTTP 409.
- Query `Get*Policy` returns AWS-shaped XML including `UserName`/`GroupName`, `PolicyName`, and RFC3986 URL-encoded `PolicyDocument`. `List*Policies` sorts policy names and supports `MaxItems`/`Marker` pagination with `IsTruncated` and next-marker output; missing users/groups or missing policy rows return `NoSuchEntity`.
- Added end-to-end evidence that a group inline policy written through IAM Query `PutGroupPolicy` participates in S3 authorization for an explicit IAM user's access key, and that removing the group membership returns the object read to implicit deny. This ties the Query API surface back to the existing identity-policy evaluator rather than only testing persistence.
- Independent Codex review first found three issues: whitespace-wrapped `UserName`/`GroupName`/`PolicyName` was being trimmed, aggregate quota check/upsert was not atomic, and duplicate GET Query parameter coverage was missing. Query validation now rejects raw whitespace without trim; quota read and upsert run under a per-principal Postgres transaction advisory lock; raw signed GET duplicate-parameter coverage was added. Follow-up Codex review: `APPROVED`.
- Verification: `mix format --check-formatted`; `mix compile --warnings-as-errors`; focused `mix test test/maxio/iam/query_api_test.exs test/maxio/s3/identity_policy_authz_test.exs test/maxio/policy/statement_test.exs test/maxio/policy/store_test.exs test/maxio/policy/store_optional_callbacks_test.exs --max-failures 1` -> 81 tests, 0 failures; final `mix precommit` -> 2764 tests, 0 failures (5 excluded).

## Previous Batch Update (2026-07-02 IAM Group Query API)

- IAM group lifecycle and membership management are now implemented on the IAM Query API path for root operators: `CreateGroup`, `GetGroup`, `ListGroups`, `DeleteGroup`, `AddUserToGroup`, `RemoveUserFromGroup`, and `ListGroupsForUser`. The actions preserve the existing root-only gate, SigV4 `iam` service-scope requirement, duplicate-parameter rejection, bounded POST form parsing, strict unsupported-parameter rejection, and IAM Query XML namespace output.
- Group Query XML now includes AWS-style `Path`, `GroupName`, synthetic stable `GroupId`, `Arn`, and `CreateDate`; `GetGroup` renders the `<Group>` detail plus paginated `<Users>`, while `ListGroups` and `ListGroupsForUser` render paginated `<Groups>` with `IsTruncated`/`Marker`. The current implementation explicitly supports only the root IAM group path `/`: non-root `CreateGroup Path` is rejected, and valid non-root `ListGroups PathPrefix` returns an empty page.
- Query `DeleteGroup` is strict rather than cascading: it returns `NoSuchEntity` for missing groups and `DeleteConflict` while memberships, inline group policies, or managed policy attachments remain. The existing backend/Console/CLI group deletion path keeps its previous cascading operator behavior through `delete_group/1`; Query uses the new `delete_group_strict/1`.
- Group names are now case-insensitive unique at both application and database boundaries through a `lower(group_name)` unique index and changeset mapping. Query membership actions strictly resolve both user and group rows: duplicate `AddUserToGroup` is a no-op success, while missing users/groups or removing a non-member returns `NoSuchEntity`.
- A separate Codex design lane required strict delete, case-insensitive group-name uniqueness, AWS-shaped group XML/pagination, strict missing-resource membership semantics, and GET/POST coverage for all seven group actions. Independent Codex review found one High issue: case-insensitive `GetGroup`/`ListGroupsForUser` resolved the canonical row but listed memberships through the originally requested casing, which could return empty membership pages. The backend list functions now canonicalize through `fetch_group_record/1` / `fetch_user_record/1` and query by stored ARN; a mixed-case Query regression covers the fix. Follow-up Codex review: `APPROVED`.
- Verification: `mix compile --warnings-as-errors`; focused `mix test test/maxio/iam/query_api_test.exs` -> 16 tests, 0 failures; `mix test test/maxio/iam_test.exs` -> 30 tests, 0 failures; adjacent IAM/Console/STS batch `mix test test/maxio/iam/query_api_test.exs test/maxio/iam_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_iam_user_group_test.exs test/maxio_web/live/group_live_test.exs test/maxio_web/live/user_live_test.exs test/maxio/sts/query_api_test.exs --max-failures 1` -> 132 tests, 0 failures; final `mix precommit` -> 2759 tests, 0 failures (5 excluded).

## Previous Batch Update (2026-07-02 IAM User Query API)

- IAM user lifecycle is now implemented on the IAM Query API path for root operators: `CreateUser`, `GetUser`, `ListUsers`, and `DeleteUser`, preserving the existing SigV4 `iam` service-scope gate, duplicate-parameter rejection, bounded POST form parsing, structured IAM XML namespace output, and fail-closed unsupported-parameter handling.
- User tags are implemented through `TagUser`, `UntagUser`, and `ListUserTags`. Create/Get responses render sorted `Tags`; ListUsers intentionally renders only user summaries. Tag validation is shared with `Maxio.Tags`, rejects `aws:` reserved prefixes and invalid lengths/characters, and now also rejects case-insensitive duplicate keys while preserving incoming key casing on overwrite.
- User Query XML now includes AWS-style `Path`, `UserName`, synthetic stable `UserId`, `Arn`, and `CreateDate`. The current implementation explicitly supports only the root IAM path `/`: non-root `CreateUser Path` is rejected, valid non-root `ListUsers PathPrefix` returns an empty page, and `PermissionsBoundary` support is implemented by the latest IAM PermissionsBoundary batch.
- Query `DeleteUser` is strict rather than cascading: it returns `DeleteConflict` while the user still has access keys, group memberships, direct inline policies, managed policy attachments, or MFA devices. Existing Console/CLI deletion keeps its previous cascading operator behavior through the separate backend path.
- A separate Codex design lane required strict delete, root-only management, case-insensitive user-name duplicate prevention, case-insensitive tag key overwrite/delete, and AWS-shaped XML/pagination. Independent Codex review found two issues: stale explicit-user access keys/sessions could remain usable if the user row disappeared, and case-insensitive username uniqueness was only an application preflight. Both were fixed by centralizing explicit-user principal usability, making credential reads and STS issuing fail closed on missing/inactive explicit users while preserving legacy self-ARN access keys, and adding a DB `lower(user_name)` unique index with changeset mapping. Follow-up Codex review: `APPROVED`.
- Verification: `mix compile --warnings-as-errors`; focused `mix test test/maxio/iam/query_api_test.exs --max-failures 1` -> 13 tests, 0 failures; IAM core `mix test test/maxio/iam_test.exs --max-failures 1` -> 29 tests, 0 failures; adjacent IAM/STS/Console batch `mix test test/maxio/iam/query_api_test.exs test/maxio/iam_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_iam_user_group_test.exs test/maxio/sts/query_api_test.exs test/maxio/sts_test.exs test/maxio_web/live/user_live_test.exs test/maxio_web/live/group_live_test.exs --max-failures 1` -> 156 tests, 0 failures; `mix format --check-formatted` passed; `git diff --check` passed with only the known STATUS CRLF normalization warning; final `mix precommit` -> 2755 tests, 0 failures (5 excluded).

## Previous Batch Update (2026-07-02 OIDC Provider Tags Query API)

- IAM OpenID Connect provider tags are now first-class in the IAM Query API. `CreateOpenIDConnectProvider` accepts `Tags.member.N.Key/Value` and returns sorted `Tags` in the create response; `GetOpenIDConnectProvider` also returns sorted tags.
- Added `TagOpenIDConnectProvider`, `UntagOpenIDConnectProvider`, and `ListOpenIDConnectProviderTags` to `Maxio.IAM.QueryPlug`, preserving the existing root-only + SigV4 `iam` scope gate, strict unsupported-parameter rejection, contiguous indexed parameter parsing, and IAM XML namespace output.
- Tag validation is centralized through `Maxio.Tags` on both Query input and `OIDCProvider` changesets. The implementation fail-closes duplicate keys, `aws:` reserved prefixes, invalid characters, bad lengths, missing Key/Value members, more than 50 tags, noncontiguous indexes, invalid `TagKeys`, and invalid `MaxItems`; tag merge overwrites same-key values and untag ignores missing keys.
- `ListOpenIDConnectProviderTags` now sorts by key and supports `MaxItems`/`Marker` pagination, returning `IsTruncated=true` and the next marker when the requested page is partial. Structured IAM logs include provider ARN and tag counts without exposing tag secrets or token material.
- Independent Codex review found two AWS compatibility gaps: create response tags were missing and `ListOpenIDConnectProviderTags` allowed but ignored pagination. Both were fixed; follow-up Codex review reported no blocking findings.
- Verification: `mix compile --warnings-as-errors`; focused `mix test test/maxio/iam/query_api_test.exs --max-failures 1` -> 10 tests, 0 failures; adjacent OIDC/STS batch `mix test test/maxio/iam/query_api_test.exs test/maxio/iam_oidc_provider_test.exs test/maxio/sts/query_api_test.exs --max-failures 1` -> 86 tests, 0 failures; final `mix precommit` -> 2750 tests, 0 failures (5 excluded).

## Previous Batch Update (2026-07-01 OIDC TLS Thumbprints)

- IAM OIDC provider management now records and enforces TLS certificate-chain thumbprint evidence on the management/refresh path. `Maxio.IAM.OIDCDiscovery.HTTP` collects top-intermediate certificate SHA1 thumbprints for both the issuer discovery endpoint and the JWKS endpoint, while keeping STS WebIdentity verification strictly on the persisted provider snapshot with no network IO.
- `CreateOpenIDConnectProvider` now treats `ThumbprintList` as optional: when omitted, discovery thumbprints are persisted automatically; when supplied, the configured list must cover every discovered issuer/JWKS thumbprint or creation fails closed. `UpdateOpenIDConnectProviderThumbprint` remains the only operation that replaces the stored thumbprint list.
- Configured `ThumbprintList` is now passed into OIDC discovery and JWKS refresh as a bounded TLS trust fallback: public-CA endpoints still use normal `Req` verification first, while private/non-public CA endpoints may complete only when the selected certificate-chain thumbprint matches the stored/configured list. The fallback is management-path only and keeps bounded JSON reads, timeout handling, and fail-closed mismatch semantics.
- DB-backed JWKS refresh now validates discovery thumbprints against the stored provider thumbprints. Mismatch is reported as `:oidc_discovery_thumbprint_mismatch` and leaves the last known good JWKS untouched; dry-run/confirmed refresh and scheduler paths share the same gate.
- Independent Codex review found three issues and all are now addressed: `ThumbprintList` participates in TLS trust fallback for private/non-public CAs; IAM Query discovery communication failures now return `OpenIdIdpCommunicationError` instead of `InvalidInput`; thumbprint fallback no longer bypasses hostname validation because the leaf certificate hostname is checked explicitly before accepting the selected thumbprint.
- Verification: `mix compile --warnings-as-errors`; private-CA discovery + wrong-host coverage `mix test test/maxio/iam/oidc_discovery/http_test.exs --max-failures 1` -> 3 tests, 0 failures; focused thumbprint `mix test test/maxio/iam/oidc_thumbprint_test.exs --max-failures 1` -> 4 tests, 0 failures; adjacent OIDC/STS batch `mix test test/maxio/iam/oidc_thumbprint_test.exs test/maxio/iam/oidc_discovery/http_test.exs test/maxio/iam_oidc_provider_test.exs test/maxio/iam_oidc_provider_refresh_test.exs test/maxio/iam/query_api_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_iam_oidc_refresh_jwks_test.exs test/maxio/sts/query_api_test.exs test/maxio/sts_test.exs --max-failures 1` -> 132 tests, 0 failures. Follow-up Codex review reported no remaining blocking findings; residual notes are multi-intermediate wrong-host coverage and the intentionally minimal fallback HTTP framing surface. Final `mix precommit` -> 2749 tests, 0 failures (5 excluded).

## Previous Batch Update (2026-07-01 WebIdentity Claims)

- `AssumeRoleWithWebIdentity` now consumes AWS OIDC JWT claims for SourceIdentity and session tags instead of accepting AssumeRole-only Query parameters. `https://aws.amazon.com/source_identity` maps into `sts:SourceIdentity` / `aws:SourceIdentity` and is persisted on the STS session; AWS nested `https://aws.amazon.com/tags` and flattened `https://aws.amazon.com/tags/principal_tags/*` / `https://aws.amazon.com/tags/transitive_tag_keys` claims map into session tags and transitive tag keys.
- `Maxio.STS.assume_role_with_web_identity/3` rebuilds the trust-policy request context from normalized claims in the core layer. A SourceIdentity claim requires the role trust policy to allow `sts:SetSourceIdentity`; any session tags require `sts:TagSession`; WebIdentity continues to have no caller access-key identity-policy side. Invalid tag claims, duplicate tag keys, transitive keys without a matching session tag, invalid SourceIdentity, and unsupported Query parameters fail closed before issuing credentials.
- WebIdentity role sessions now persist `source_identity`, `tags`, and `transitive_tag_keys`; `Maxio.Auth.CredentialStore.EctoStore` already restores those fields into the S3 principal context, so role policies can use `aws:SourceIdentity` and `aws:PrincipalTag/*`. The STS XML response now includes `<SourceIdentity>` when present, and structured STS logs include source identity and tag keys without raw tokens or raw subjects.
- Independent Codex design review found one must-fix: WebIdentity SourceIdentity validation missed AWS's allowed `+` character. The regex and end-to-end test were fixed; follow-up Codex review found no runtime issues and its low-severity doc drift was fixed. Added fail-closed Query API coverage for invalid JWT SourceIdentity/tag claim shapes. Verification: `mix compile --warnings-as-errors`; focused `mix test test/maxio/sts/query_api_test.exs test/maxio/sts_test.exs --max-failures 1` -> 98 tests, 0 failures; adjacent STS/S3 policy batch `mix test test/maxio/sts/query_api_test.exs test/maxio/sts_test.exs test/maxio/s3/identity_policy_authz_test.exs test/maxio/s3/authz_test.exs test/maxio/policy/condition_test.exs test/maxio/tags_test.exs --max-failures 1` -> 153 tests, 0 failures; final `mix precommit` -> 2739 tests, 0 failures (5 excluded).

## Previous Batch Update (2026-07-01 OIDC JWKS Refresh)

- IAM OIDC provider JWKS refresh is now implemented as a DB snapshot maintenance path, not an STS request-path network fetch. `Maxio.IAM.refresh_oidc_provider_jwks/2` refreshes one provider, `refresh_oidc_provider_jwks_if_due/1` refreshes due active providers, and both validate remote discovery/JWKS through the persisted `OIDCProvider` changeset so private/symmetric key material cannot be written.
- The operator CLI `mix maxio.iam.oidc.refresh_jwks (--all | --provider ARN | --issuer URL) [--confirm] [--json]` is dry-run by default. Confirmed runs update `jwks`, `jwks_fetched_at`, and `discovery_document`; discovery/JWKS failures leave the last known good DB snapshot unchanged and report sanitized errors without JWK material.
- The disabled-by-default `Maxio.IAM.OIDCProviderRefresh.Scheduler` is wired into supervision and `config :maxio, :iam_oidc_provider_jwks_refresh`. Scheduler writes require `confirm_refresh: true`; `run_opts: [dry_run: false]` cannot bypass that gate. STS WebIdentity verification continues to use only stored provider snapshots and does not call discovery on `kid` miss / invalid signature.
- Independent Codex review found and this batch fixed two issues: due filtering now happens before `limit` so fresh providers cannot starve stale provider refresh, and logger default metadata now includes OIDC provider identity fields. Follow-up Codex review approved with no actionable findings. Verification: `mix compile --warnings-as-errors`; `mix test test/maxio/iam_oidc_provider_refresh_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_iam_oidc_refresh_jwks_test.exs --max-failures 1` -> 11 tests, 0 failures; adjacent OIDC/IAM/STS batch `mix test test/maxio/iam_oidc_provider_refresh_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_iam_oidc_refresh_jwks_test.exs test/maxio/iam_oidc_provider_test.exs test/maxio/iam/query_api_test.exs test/maxio/sts/query_api_test.exs test/maxio/sts_test.exs --max-failures 1` -> 118 tests, 0 failures; final `mix precommit` -> 2735 tests, 0 failures (5 excluded).

## Previous Batch Update (2026-07-01 IdP Console)

- IdP Console management is now implemented for IAM OIDC providers. The root-only LiveView page is mounted at `/idp` and supports provider create/delete, select/detail, status update, client ID add/remove, thumbprint replacement, and JWKS replacement without pre-rendering current key material.
- OIDC provider JWKS validation now accepts only public JWK material for persisted providers and rejects private/symmetric key fields. IAM public projections and STS WebIdentity provider lookup sanitize JWKS through the same public-key projection, including legacy bad rows.
- Independent Codex review found no actionable remaining findings after the root-only, invalid-status fail-closed, and public-JWKS fixes. Follow-up residual test gaps were also closed for forged create status and EC/OKP private JWK rejection.
- Verification: `mix compile --warnings-as-errors`; focused `mix test test/maxio_web/live/idp_live_test.exs test/maxio/iam_oidc_provider_test.exs --max-failures 1` -> 14 tests, 0 failures; adjacent Console/IAM/STS batch `mix test test/maxio_web/live/idp_live_test.exs test/maxio_web/live/role_live_test.exs test/maxio_web/live/sts_session_live_test.exs test/maxio_web/live/user_live_test.exs test/maxio_web/live/group_live_test.exs test/maxio_web/live/shell_test.exs test/maxio/iam_oidc_provider_test.exs test/maxio/iam/query_api_test.exs test/maxio/sts/query_api_test.exs --max-failures 1` -> 128 tests, 0 failures; final `mix precommit` -> 2724 tests, 0 failures (5 excluded).
