## 当前续作进展（2026-06-12：M8 quorum lock core）

- **M8-1 dsync-style 锁核心落地**：新增 `Maxio.Cluster.LockServer`，每节点持有本地 lease table；`Maxio.Cluster.Lock` 的公共 `with_lock/2,3` / `quorum_with_lock/2,3` 调用面保持不变，但实现改为 quorum lease acquire/release/refresh/force_unlock。单节点退化为 quorum=1，同一代码路径；读锁可共享，写锁独占，owner 进程死亡与 monotonic TTL sweep 都会释放本地 token。临界区在受监控 worker 中运行，heartbeat 一旦丢 quorum 会中止 worker 并返回既有锁失败形态，避免 lease 已失效但写入继续。
- **写路径兼容性**：`Maxio.Lock.with_object_lock/2` 与所有 S3 handler 调用点无需改动；锁竞争仍映射 `503 RequestTimeout`，写 quorum/rename 争用仍映射 `SlowDownWrite`。旧测试里通过 `:global.set_lock` 伪造竞争的路径已改为后台进程持有 `Lock.acquire/2` 的同资源 lease。
- **诚实边界**：这是 dsync/Redlock 类 best-effort quorum lock，不是 Raft 线性化锁；节点重启会遗忘本地 lease，真正强写 epoch / stale writer 拒绝仍需要后续 fencing token 写入 sidecar/元数据。M8-2 跨节点 cache invalidation 与 M8-3 自动 distributed scanner/heal 未在本轮实现。
- **验证**：`mix compile --warnings-as-errors` 通过；`mix test test/maxio/cluster/lock_test.exs` → 12 passed；锁+写路径扩展批次 `mix test test/maxio/s3/seam_consistency_test.exs test/maxio/s3/slow_down_quorum_test.exs test/maxio/storage/distributed_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/lock_test.exs` → 27 passed；`git diff --check` 通过。独立 `gpt-5.5` 只读 gap assessment 确认 M8-1 是当前最高价值 bounded slice；首轮独立 `gpt-5.5` code review 发现 lease-loss 未中止临界区、timed-out acquire 清理不完整、heartbeat monitor 泄漏，均已修复并补回归；复审发现 expired refresh 可复活 lease，已修复为 refresh 前强制 sweep 并补回归。

## 当前续作进展（2026-06-12：M8 cache invalidation spine）

- **M8-2 跨节点 metadata 失效管线落地**：新增 `Maxio.Cluster.Notify`，统一通过 `Phoenix.PubSub` topic `maxio:metadata` 广播 metadata invalidation event；新增 `Maxio.Cluster.CacheInvalidator` 并挂入监督树（位于 PubSub 之后），每节点订阅同 topic。`Maxio.Auth.CredentialStore` 增加可选 `invalidate/1` callback，`MemoryStore` 对 `{:access_key, ak}` 做 ETS 删除、`:all` 做全量刷新，`EctoStore` 当前 no-op（生产 EctoStore 每请求读 Postgres，无本地 credential cache）。
- **已接入 mutation 点**：IAM access key create/delete（含幂等 delete）广播 `{:iam_changed, ak}`；bucket config put/delete（CORS/Lifecycle/Encryption/PublicAccessBlock，含幂等 delete）广播 `{:bucket_config_changed, bucket, kind}`；bucket metadata create/delete/ACL/tags/versioning 广播 `{:bucket_metadata_changed, bucket}`；bucket/identity policy facade put/delete 广播 bucket/principal policy event；对象 ACL/tag 更新广播 `{:object_metadata_changed, bucket, key, version_id}`。接线在 context/facade 层，S3 handler、Console LiveView、Mix 任务共享同一失效路径。
- **诚实边界**：当前生产元数据主路径仍主要依赖共享 Postgres / sidecar 权威读取，`CacheInvalidator` 除 credential MemoryStore 外多数事件是 no-op；这一步提供后续 read-through cache、跨节点 policy cache、scanner 协调的统一事件骨架，不等价于 MinIO `.minio.sys` shared-nothing 元数据复制，也不提供 Raft/epoch 级强一致。
- **验证**：`mix compile --warnings-as-errors` 通过；focused 批次 `mix test test/maxio/policy/store_optional_callbacks_test.exs test/maxio/objects_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/notify_test.exs test/maxio/auth/credential_store/memory_store_test.exs test/maxio/iam_test.exs test/maxio/bucket_configs_test.exs test/maxio/buckets_test.exs test/maxio/policy/store_test.exs` → 49 passed；S3 handler 回归 `mix test test/maxio/s3/acl_test.exs test/maxio/s3/tagging_e2e_test.exs test/maxio/s3/bucket_policy_test.exs test/maxio/s3/cors_e2e_test.exs test/maxio/s3/lifecycle_e2e_test.exs test/maxio/s3/versioning_config_test.exs test/maxio/s3/public_access_block_test.exs` → 69 passed；`git diff --check` 通过；`mix precommit` → 1081 passed, 1 excluded；独立 `gpt-5.5` review APPROVED，指出的 object metadata event 测试缺口与 optional callback guard 已补齐。

## 当前续作进展（2026-06-12：M8 PG metadata-driven scrubber）

- **M8-3a 自动扫描 / auto-heal spine 落地**：新增 `Maxio.Cluster.Scrubber`（监督树启动，默认 disabled 不调度），独立于 Route B 的 `Maxio.Storage.Scanner`。Scrubber 从 `Maxio.Objects` 有界分页枚举当前对象或全部版本，FS 后端仅刷新 `Maxio.Cluster.Scrubber.Usage` bucket 快照；`Maxio.Storage.ErasureCoded` 与 `Maxio.Storage.Distributed` 后端按 erasure set 通过 M8-1 `Maxio.Cluster.Lock.quorum_with_lock/3` 获取 heartbeat-backed 短租约后，分别复用 `Maxio.Storage.Erasure.Heal` / `Maxio.Cluster.Heal` 做完整性检测与自愈。Distributed 的默认锁节点来自对象所在 erasure set 端点节点，避免每节点本地-only 重复扫描；EC 默认本机节点。新增 `Maxio.Cluster.Scrubber.Throttle` 与配置 `config :maxio, :scrubber`（interval/page_size/scan_versions/sample_inverse/max_objects_per_sec/batch sleep/adaptive IO pressure source/lock TTL），以及 CLI `mix maxio.scan [--bucket <name>] [--versions] [--no-throttle] [--max-objects-per-sec N] [--object-burst N] [--sleep-between-batches-ms MS] [--no-adaptive-io] [--io-load-source s3-in-flight|storage-ops|drive-device-io|combined|cluster-s3-in-flight|cluster-storage-ops|cluster-drive-device-io|cluster-combined] [--max-live-requests N] [--io-pause-ms MS] [--io-max-pause-ms MS]`。
- **M8-3b PG 驱动采样补齐**：周期 scrub 轮通过 `sample_inverse` + monotonically increasing `sample_cycle` 做确定性 1/N 深度 heal 检查（同一 bucket/key/version 稳定落一个采样分区，周期轮换覆盖）；手动 `run_once` / `mix maxio.scan` 默认 `sample: :full`，继续用于全量取证。summary 增加 `sampled_out`，usage 仍对当前对象全量统计，不因深度采样而低估容量。
- **M8-3c operational scan 集成**：`Maxio.Cluster.Scrubber.run_once/1` 增加 opt-in `reconcile_index: true` pre-pass，先调用既有 `Maxio.Storage.Scanner.run_once/1` 做 disk/peer sidecar → PG 派生索引收敛，再用刷新后的 PG inventory 做 usage/heal。summary 与 `mix maxio.scan` 输出新增 `index_status/index_scanned/index_upserted/index_gc/index_skipped/index_divergent`；默认不越过 `authoritative_source(:write)` 安全闸，CLI 需显式 `--force-reconcile-index` 才接受盘权威。FS backend override 会注入同源 local topology，分布式调用继续透传 `topology`/`transport`。
- **M8-3d/e orphan shard evidence + guarded cleanup**：`Maxio.Storage.Scanner` 的同轮 drive-walk 增加孤儿分片取证：`ShardServer.list_dir/2` 新增 opt-in `include_layout_files`（默认调用路径仍只返回目录），scanner 只在显式取证路径看到 `meta.json`/`part.0` 文件；检测 `part.0` 无 sidecar 与 delete-marker 版本目录残留 `part.0`，输出/日志 `orphan_shards/orphan_bytes`，并经 `Scrubber` 透传为 `index_orphan_shards/index_orphan_bytes`。清理默认关闭；显式 `--cleanup-orphan-shards` / `--orphan-cleanup-dry-run` 时，经 sidecar 写权威、全端点可达、对象锁、二次探针、年龄门（默认 48h，可 CLI 覆盖）与每轮删除上限（默认 100）后，仅删除 `part.0`，绝不删除目录或 `meta.json`，并输出 `orphan_cleaned/orphan_cleaned_bytes` / `index_orphan_cleaned/index_orphan_cleaned_bytes`。
- **M8-4 integration evidence / peer harness**：新增 `test/maxio/cluster/m8_integration_test.exs`，在默认回归中用 `Distributed` + `InProcess` + local-node topology 证明监督树内 `Scrubber.scan_now/1` 可读取 PG inventory、经 set-resource lock 调用 `ClusterHeal` 修复缺失 shard，并在同一流程验证 bucket/object metadata mutation 会经 `Notify` topic 广播；同文件还证明 set lock 被持有时 scrubber 计 `skipped` 且不越权 heal，释放后下一轮才修复。新增 `test/maxio/cluster/peer_lock_test.exs`（`@tag :distributed` 默认排除，且需 `MAXIO_RUN_PEER_LOCK=1`）作为真 `:peer` 证据 harness：可起 peer BEAM 节点并启动 peer `LockServer`/`ShardServer` 时，验证 `Lock.acquire/2` 经 `:erpc` 跨节点排他、3 节点丢 1 peer 后仍可取得多数 quorum，以及 peer-backed scrubber 默认 set-node lock 选择。未设置环境变量时该文件报告 skipped，避免把不可用 peer 环境伪装成通过。
- **M8 pressure counters + cached cluster aggregation**：`Maxio.S3.InFlight` 由长期 GenServer 持有 node-local ETS，`S3.Plugs.Dispatch` 包裹 handler 生命周期，正常返回、结构化 S3 错误和异常路径都会释放计数。`Maxio.Cluster.DriveIO` 由监督树持有 node-local ETS，`Maxio.Cluster.ShardServer` 的 shard/sidecar/list/probe/stat/delete/commit/heal 路径都经 fail-open counter 包裹，记录 active storage ops、读写/metadata/delete 次数、completed attempts、best-effort bytes、应用层 latency（`max_latency_us` 为并发完成下的 best-effort 高水位）与自上次 reset 起的吞吐估算；`mix maxio.drive.io [--reset]` 可打印/清零本节点累计 telemetry，reset 保留 active gauges 以免干扰 adaptive pacing。新增 `Maxio.Cluster.DriveStats` / `mix maxio.drive.stats [--summary-only] [--drive <path>]`，经 `Transport.drive_stats/3` 读取 topology endpoint filesystem capacity/free/used、mount_root、source、precision 与最小 free-space；默认 provider 使用 OTP `os_mon`/`disksup`，可经 `config :maxio, :drive_stats, provider: fun/1` 注入。新增 `Maxio.Cluster.DriveDeviceIO` / `mix maxio.drive.device_io [--summary-only] [--drive <path>] [--cached] [--reset]`，监督树内缓存本节点 drive root 的 OS/device pressure；Linux 默认 provider 读取 `/proc/self/mountinfo` + `/proc/diskstats`，非 Linux、provider 错误/异常、无 topology 或 stale cache 全部 fail-open 为 0。新增 `Maxio.Cluster.PressureAggregator` 通过 `Transport.pressure_snapshot/2` 后台缓存 peer `IOPressure.local_snapshot/0`，对 scrubber/fresh-drive hot path 只提供本地计数 + 新鲜缓存聚合；stale/down/timeout peer fail-open。
- **Scrubber adaptive IO gate**：`Maxio.Cluster.Scrubber.Throttle` 在 token-bucket object pacing 后追加 adaptive IO gate；`adaptive_io: true` 时，所选 `io_load_source`（`s3-in-flight` / `storage-ops` / `drive-device-io` / `combined` / `cluster-s3-in-flight` / `cluster-storage-ops` / `cluster-drive-device-io` / `cluster-combined`，默认 `cluster-combined`）超过 `max_live_requests` 会让 scrubber 在对象之间按 `io_pause_ms` 分段等待，单对象最多 `io_max_pause_ms`；`--no-throttle` 同时关闭 token/fixed/adaptive sleeps。
- **M8 drive health / 长窗口离线观测 + software drive identity**：`Maxio.Cluster.DriveHealth` 监督树 registry 按 `{node, drive}` 记录结构化 reachability probe，单次失败进入 `suspect`，超过 `offline_after_ms`（默认 48h，可 `--drive-offline-after-ms` 覆盖）才进入 `offline`；若离线期间 erasure set 仍达到 write quorum，恢复后保留 `heal_required?` 标记。新增 `Maxio.Cluster.DriveIdentity`，每个本机 drive root 有 `.maxio-drive-identity.json` 软件 UUID marker，并用 `cluster_drive_identities`（中文 COMMENT 自文档化 schema）按 `{node_name, drive_path}` 持久化最近观察到的 UUID；同一路径看到新 UUID 时记录 `previous_drive_uuid`、递增 generation、置 `identity_heal_required`，并把该信号折入 `DriveHealth.heal_required?`。`Transport.probe_drive_identity/3` / `ShardServer.probe_drive_identity/2` 使 `Maxio.Cluster.Scrubber` 每轮 Distributed 扫描按 topology 同时探测 reachability 与 identity；summary / `mix maxio.scan` 输出增加 `drive_identity_replacements/drive_identity_heal_required/drive_identity_errors`。
- **M8 fresh-drive heal operator workflow**：新增 `Maxio.Cluster.FreshDriveHeal` 与 `mix maxio.heal.fresh_drives`。该显式运维入口读取 `DriveHealth.heal_required?`，每个 erasure set 每轮最多处理一个已恢复 drive，枚举 PG inventory 中落到 affected set 的对象/版本（默认含 delete marker；优先对象持久化 placement，旧行回退 router），并复用 `Maxio.Cluster.Heal.heal_object/4` + set-resource quorum lock 修复缺失 shard 或 marker sidecar。仅当完整、非 bucket 限定、非 current-only、非 dry-run、未被 `--max-objects` 截断，且 missing/skipped/unrecoverable/error 全为 0 时，才清除已处理 drive 的 runtime `heal_required?` 与 persistent `identity_heal_required` marker。
- **M8 fresh-drive heal scheduler**：新增 `Maxio.Cluster.FreshDriveHeal.Scheduler`，监督树启动但默认 disabled。启用 `config :maxio, :fresh_drive_heal_scheduler, enabled: true` 后按 `interval_ms` 在 cluster singleton lock 下运行；每轮可先 `DriveHealth.probe_topology/1` 刷新 marker，再调用同一个 `FreshDriveHeal.run_once/1` workflow。`run_now/1` 暴露给 IEx/测试取证；手动 `mix maxio.heal.fresh_drives` 同步接入 scrubber throttle flags（含 `--io-load-source`），自动/手动入口共享 token-bucket + adaptive IO pacing。`auto_rebalance_after_heal: true` 默认关闭；显式开启后，只有 clean 且实际清 marker 的 pass 才通过 `Rebalance.Jobs.start_rebalance_if_needed/1` enqueue 普通 rebalance job，且仅当 PG placement 与当前 router 有 `routing_mismatches` 时创建；placement fallback、active job 与其它失败均不让 heal 成功回滚，而是写入 scheduler report 的 `rebalance_enqueue` 证据。`auto_advance_rebalance_after_heal: true` 同样默认关闭；开启后只对本轮创建的同一个普通 rebalance job 调普通 `Rebalance.Scheduler.run_steps/1`，受 `rebalance_advance_max_steps` 限制，可 opt-in closure verifier，所有结果写入 `rebalance_advance` 证据。
- **M8 replacement workflow runbook/auditor**：新增 `Maxio.Cluster.Replacement.Workflow` 与 `mix maxio.replacement.workflow`。默认只读审计 `DriveHealth` heal-required 摘要、latest rebalance workflow evidence 与 next safe action；`--probe` 才刷新 drive probes，`--heal` 才调用 fresh-drive scheduler bridge。`--rebalance-if-needed`、`--advance-steps N` 与 `--verify-closure` 只在同一次显式 `--heal` run 中生效，且复用既有 enqueue / same-job bounded drain / closure verifier；不接受任意 job_id advance，不执行 decommission、topology mutation、deployment finalization、physical remove 或 purge。`--fail-on-blocked` 可把 blocked/failed 报告变成输出后非零 gate；该 CLI 使用最小 runtime 启动，不 `app.start`，避免 operator 环境中已启用的后台 scheduler 在演练子进程里并发运行。`Maxio.Cluster.ReplacementLiveRehearsal` / `scripts/m8_replacement_live_rehearsal.exs` 可把 workflow JSON 归档成 artifact，并要求 `blockers=[]` 与 `next_action=none` 才通过；默认 `--dry-run` 不通过 live gate，除非显式 `--allow-dry-run-success` 作为非 live 证据归档。
- **M8 scheduler run ledger / backoff / alert boundary + cluster-cadence evidence v1 + cadence/overdue observer v1 + durable rollup/prune/retention contract**：新增 `cluster_scheduler_runs`、`cluster_scheduler_states` 与 `cluster_scheduler_run_rollups`（表、列、索引均中文 COMMENT），`Maxio.Cluster.SchedulerRuns` 持久化 fresh-drive heal scheduler 与 rebalance scheduler 每次手动 `run_now/1` / 后台 scheduled pass 的结果，并支持把已关闭的 scheduler/trigger/time window 汇总成幂等 durable rollup，再由显式 operator prune 删除已复核明细。`mix maxio.scheduler.status [--scheduler fresh_drive_heal|rebalance] [--trigger manual|scheduled] [--runs N] [--state-only] [--cadence] [--rollups] [--rollup-limit N] [--since-minutes N] [--expected-interval-ms N] [--stale-after-ms N] [--json]` 可查询 run ledger、effective health、连续失败、退避窗口、告警状态、scheduled-only cadence health、node 参与度/lock loser 证据与 rollup 摘要；`mix maxio.scheduler.evidence [--scheduler fresh_drive_heal|rebalance] [--from <iso8601> --until <iso8601>|--since-minutes N] [--expected-nodes node_a,node_b] [--min-effective-nodes N] [--require-lock-loser] [--require-rollups] [--fail-on-attention] [--json]` 可把长跑窗口转成只读 gate artifact；`docs/superpowers/runbooks/m8_scheduler_live_rehearsal.md` 与 `scripts/m8_scheduler_live_rehearsal.exs` 把真实多节点长跑演练收口为只读 evidence gate + JSON artifact；`mix maxio.scheduler.rollup --from <iso8601> --until <iso8601> [--scheduler fresh_drive_heal|rebalance] [--trigger manual|scheduled] [--window-minutes N] [--dry-run] [--json]` 写入 rollup，半开区间 `[start, end)`、UTC epoch-aligned window，默认只接受关闭窗口；`mix maxio.scheduler.prune --scheduler <name> --trigger manual|scheduled --from <iso8601> --until <iso8601> --window-minutes N --dry-run` 只做 preflight，真实删除必须追加 `--confirm-prune --confirm-scheduler <same> --confirm-trigger <same> --confirm-from <same> --confirm-until <same> --confirm-window-minutes <same>`；`mix maxio.scheduler.retention --scheduler <name> --trigger manual|scheduled --retain-days N --window-minutes N --dry-run` 只规划默认关闭的旧 raw run retention，真实删除必须显式 `--enable-retention --confirm-retention` 并确认 scheduler/trigger/retain-days/window/cutoff。`--cadence` 与 evidence gate 均只读聚合 run/state，不触发 scheduler/runner；evidence CLI 只启动 Repo、不 `app.start`，不写 DB；`--fail-on-attention` 只把 warnings 级别的 blocked/failed 历史证据提升为 CLI 非零退出，供长跑演练 gate 使用。manual run 不能证明后台 scheduler 存活，`lock_unavailable`/backoff 等 skipped scheduled row 只计证据窗口但不能变绿，latest scheduled `failed/blocked` 优先于 manual recovery，且忽略 `finished_at > now` 的未来 run；evidence gate 使用 `[from, until)` 半开窗口，与 rollup/retention 对齐，expected node 缺席、effective node 不足、要求 lock-loser 却缺证据、或 `--require-rollups` 缺 scheduled rollup 均 fail-closed。`no_runnable_job` 归类 idle，`lock_unavailable`/backoff skip 归类 skipped，blocked/failed 才推进连续失败计数，且 neutral coordination skip 只写入独立 skipped run evidence，不覆盖已有 effective state。达到 `scheduler_ledger.failure_threshold` 后，自动 scheduled pass 在 `backoff_ms` 窗口内跳过 runner；手动 `run_now/1` 仍可用于 operator 修复。alert sink 失败只注记本次 run summary 的 `alert_emit_error`，不让 scheduler 崩溃；rebalance closure blocked `next_action` 会进入 redacted/bounded run summary，供 operator 闭环修复。 本地 cadence 测试已覆盖 fresh-drive/rebalance 两个 scheduler 同轮竞争只有一个 runner 进入；peer cadence 取证入口 env-gated，未配置时 skip 避免假绿。
- **诚实边界**：对象 heal inventory 仍来自 PG 元数据索引；开启 `reconcile_index` 时可先把 disk/peer sidecar 权威收敛进 PG，再 heal。orphan cleanup 是 opt-in guarded byte cleanup；adaptive pacing 当前读取本节点 S3 handler in-flight、ShardServer active storage-op、DriveDeviceIO OS/device pressure 与缓存 peer snapshot，并在对象之间暂停；`DriveIO` latency/throughput 是 ShardServer 应用层观测，`DriveStats` 是 filesystem capacity/free-space 观测，`DriveDeviceIO` 是 fail-open cached pressure 观测（Linux 默认走 `/proc/diskstats`，非 Linux/错误/stale 全为 0），三者都不提供 SMART、硬件序列号、硬件 service time、物理吞吐保证或 admission control；`DriveHardwareEvidence --smart` 只提供默认关闭的 SMART allowlist 子证据，缺失/权限/timeout/invalid JSON 均 fail-open 到 evidence，不改对象路由/quorum，也不是硬盘健康保证、secure erase 证明或物理 replacement identity。DriveStats 默认不改对象路由，显式启用 pool write policy 时仅参与普通新写的 pool 加权落点，不迁移旧对象、不做 rebalance。跨节点部分是 stale-aware/fail-open cache，不是 admission control 或同步负载仲裁。DriveHealth + DriveIdentity 当前只提供 node-local 观测与 same-path software UUID replacement detection，不改 S3 请求路由/quorum；fresh-drive scheduler 的 rebalance bridge 默认只创建普通 rebalance control-plane job；显式开启 bounded drain 或 replacement workflow advance 时也只复用既有 rebalance runner/source-cleanup/closure verifier，不做 decommission、topology mutation、deployment finalization、physical remove/purge 或新的 copy/delete logic；replacement workflow 是 operator runbook/auditor，不是生产级全自动 replacement policy。SchedulerRuns 只记录、分类、退避、effective health、scheduled-only overdue 观察、durable rollup/prune/retention 与告警自动 scheduled pass，不启用 scheduler、不移动数据、不越过 operator 修复 blocker；cluster-cadence evidence v1 证明 singleton 竞争语义，不等同于长期生产自动化策略；prune/retention 均是显式 operator action，只删除与 durable rollup count/digest/source updated_at 完全一致且不命中 state/cadence 保护的 exact raw ids，任一窗口不满足条件则整次 range fail-closed；retention 默认关闭，不自动创建 rollup、不删除 rollup rows、不接后台 timer。

skipped、`maxio.reconcile_index`/`maxio.scan` 非零 orphan 计数透传、dry-run cleanup 保留字节、真实 cleanup 仅删除 `part.0`、delete-marker cleanup 保留 `meta.json`、年轻 shard/端点不可达/PG 行存在均跳过 cleanup；独立 `Poincare` review APPROVED（两条 LOW 文档缺口已修复）；早期 `gpt-5.5` reviews 针对 lock、采样、index 输出、orphan endpoint down 的 findings 均已收敛。

## 下一步（续作入口）

- **2026-06-12 22:10 交接记录**：本轮完成 M8 orphan shard guarded cleanup。默认仍只报告孤儿 `part.0` evidence；显式 `--cleanup-orphan-shards` 才在 sidecar 写权威、全端点可达、对象锁、二次探针、年龄门、run-scoped 删除上限同时满足后删除 `part.0`，绝不删除目录或 `meta.json`。`maxio.reconcile_index` / `maxio.scan --reconcile-index` 已透传 cleanup/dry-run/min-age/max-delete 参数并输出 `orphan_cleaned*` / `index_orphan_cleaned*` 字段。独立 gpt-5.5 reviewer 先指出 `orphan_cleanup_max_deletes` 被按桶而非按轮执行的 HIGH；已修复为跨 bucket 累计 `orphan_cleanup_used`，并补双桶 `max_deletes: 1` 回归。修复后 focused 验证：`mix test test/maxio/storage/scanner_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_reconcile_index_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_scan_test.exs` → 36 passed；`mix precommit` → 1108 passed, 4 excluded；gpt-5.5 复审 APPROVED。残余风险：并发启动的多个 scanner 进程仍各自拥有一份 per-run budget，符合当前设计但需运维避免并行跑多个清理轮。提交已本地创建；push 因本仓库没有配置 remote（`git remote -v` 为空）失败，换机前需先配置远端或用其它方式转移本地提交。
- **2026-06-12 续作：M8 scanner/scrubber token-bucket 限流收口**：`Maxio.Cluster.Scrubber.Throttle` 已有 disabled/fixed/token-bucket state，`Scrubber.run_once/1` 跨 bucket/object 传递同一个 throttle state，`mix maxio.scan` 支持 `--max-objects-per-sec` / `--object-burst` / `--sleep-between-batches-ms`，`config :maxio, :scrubber` 默认 `max_objects_per_sec: 200, object_burst: 1`。本轮修复 CLI 未传值时 `nil` 覆盖配置默认值导致默认限流被关闭的问题；新增测试覆盖 burst/refill、跨 bucket state carry 与 CLI flag 接受/执行。语义说明：这是保守 object pacing，允许 token-bucket burst，随后限制下一次对象扫描开始；真实 drive IO 负载反馈、跨节点聚合仍 defer。验证：focused `mix test test/maxio/cluster/scrubber_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_scan_test.exs` → 15 passed；`mix compile --warnings-as-errors` 通过；`git diff --check` 通过；`mix precommit` → 1111 passed, 4 excluded（复跑同样通过）；独立 reviewer 复审 APPROVED，残余风险为 `--reconcile-index` pre-pass 与 `scan_versions` 的 usage pass 尚不受 object-token pacing，已作为后续边界记录。
- **2026-06-12/13 续作：M8 scanner/scrubber live IO adaptive pacing**：新增 `Maxio.S3.InFlight`，由监督树内长期 GenServer 持有 ETS，`S3.Plugs.Dispatch` 在 handler 生命周期内 track 本节点 live request 数；新增测试覆盖 `track/1` 正常/异常释放、并发计数、PUT handler 阻塞期间计数、GET chunked stream 期间计数、mapped storage error 后释放。后续追加 `Maxio.Cluster.DriveIO` + `Maxio.Cluster.IOPressure`，把 `ShardServer` active storage-op 纳入 adaptive gate；本轮再追加 `Maxio.Cluster.PressureAggregator`，默认 `cluster-combined` 通过 cached peer snapshots 聚合 S3 in-flight + storage-op pressure。`mix maxio.scan` / `mix maxio.heal.fresh_drives` 支持 `--io-load-source s3-in-flight|storage-ops|combined|cluster-s3-in-flight|cluster-storage-ops|cluster-combined`（后续 2026-06-13 的 DriveDeviceIO 续作已扩展到 `drive-device-io` / `cluster-drive-device-io`）。语义说明：这是 best-effort 的对象间暂停，不是请求拒绝/限流；当前实现也会在最后一个对象后执行一次 conservative wait；`--reconcile-index` pre-pass、`scan_versions` 的 usage pass、早期 CORS/SigV4 reject 仍是边界。验证：focused `mix test test/maxio/cluster/scrubber_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_scan_test.exs test/maxio/s3/in_flight_test.exs` → 22 passed；2026-06-13 pressure focused 批次 → 25 passed；M8 adjacent pressure/heal batch → 52 passed；`mix compile --warnings-as-errors` 通过；`git diff --check` 通过；`mix precommit` → 1152 tests, 0 failures, 4 excluded；独立 reviewer APPROVED。
- **2026-06-12 续作：M8 drive health 长窗口离线观测**：新增 `Maxio.Cluster.DriveHealth` node-local GenServer registry（监督树启动），用结构化 reachability probe 记录 `{node, drive}` 的 `online/suspect/offline` 状态；单次失败不 offline，超过 `offline_after_ms`（默认 48h）才 offline；恢复时仅当离线期间 erasure set 仍达到 write quorum 才保留 `heal_required?` fresh-drive heal 标记。`Maxio.Storage.Scanner` 在既有 reverse-GC/orphan cleanup `list_dir/3` 探针处记录健康信号；`Maxio.Cluster.Scrubber` 每轮 Distributed 扫描使用 transport root-drive probe 按 topology 探测 drive，并把 `drive_sets/drive_endpoints/drive_online/drive_suspect/drive_offline/drive_heal_required` 输出到 summary 与 `mix maxio.scan`；`test/support/transport_in_process.ex` 支持 drive-level outage，不再只能模拟整 node down。诚实边界：这是运行时观测信号，不改 S3 请求路由/quorum，也不实现自动 replacement 调度、持久化 drive identity、跨节点 consensus、真实 drive IO 负载。独立 review 首轮发现 production drive root missing 被普通 `list_dir/3` 的 `:enoent -> {:ok, []}` 语义掩盖；已新增 `Transport.probe_drive/2` / `ShardServer.probe_drive/1` 并补 missing-root 回归。验证：focused `mix test test/maxio/cluster/drive_health_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/transport_in_process_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/m8_integration_test.exs test/maxio/storage/scanner_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_scan_test.exs` → 47 passed；`mix compile --warnings-as-errors` 通过；`mix precommit` → 1126 passed, 4 excluded。
- **2026-06-12 续作：M8 fresh-drive heal operator workflow**：新增 `Maxio.Cluster.FreshDriveHeal` 和 `mix maxio.heal.fresh_drives`，消费 DriveHealth 恢复后的 `heal_required?` marker。workflow 按 affected erasure set 过滤 PG inventory，默认扫描所有版本和 delete marker；数据对象仍走 EC shard heal，delete-marker sidecar 走 metadata-only restore 并删除残留 `part.0`，绝不伪造对象字节。marker clear 条件保守：必须全局完整 pass、非 bucket 限定、非 current-only、非 dry-run、未被 `max_objects` 截断，并且 missing/skipped/unrecoverable/errors 全 0；同一 set 多个 marker 每轮只清一个，其余 defer 到后续 pass。验证：focused `mix test test/maxio/cluster/heal_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/fresh_drive_heal_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_heal_fresh_drives_test.exs` → 15 passed；相邻 M8 批次 `mix test test/maxio/cluster/m8_integration_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/scrubber_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/drive_health_test.exs` → 21 passed；`mix compile --warnings-as-errors` 通过；`mix precommit` → 1137 tests, 0 failures, 4 excluded。
- **2026-06-13 续作：M8 fresh-drive heal scheduler**：新增 `Maxio.Cluster.FreshDriveHeal.Scheduler` 并接入监督树，默认 disabled；启用后按 `interval_ms` 通过 singleton quorum lock 自动运行 marker-based fresh-drive heal workflow，并可选先执行 `DriveHealth.probe_topology/1`。`Scheduler.run_now/1` 可手动触发并记录 `last_result` / `last_skip_reason`；runner/probe 可注入以测试调度、锁和异常捕获。`FreshDriveHeal` 本体复用 `Maxio.Cluster.Scrubber.Throttle`，手动 CLI 新增 `--no-throttle`、`--max-objects-per-sec`、`--object-burst`、`--sleep-between-batches-ms`、`--no-adaptive-io`、`--io-load-source`、`--max-live-requests`、`--io-pause-ms`、`--io-max-pause-ms`。诚实边界：这是对现有 `heal_required?` marker 的自动消费入口，不提供持久化 job queue、物理 drive identity、replacement UUID 跟踪、跨节点 consensus 或 rebalance/decommission。验证：相邻 M8 批次 `mix test test/maxio/cluster/fresh_drive_heal_scheduler_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/fresh_drive_heal_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_heal_fresh_drives_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/m8_integration_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/scrubber_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/drive_health_test.exs` → 40 passed；`mix compile --warnings-as-errors` 通过；`git diff --check` 通过；`mix precommit` → 1146 tests, 0 failures, 4 excluded；独立 code review APPROVED，确认 scheduler 不自行清 marker、singleton lock fail-closed、异常被记录且进程保持、throttle accumulator 不破坏 cap semantics。

- **2026-06-13 续作：M8 drive filesystem capacity stats CLI**：新增 `Maxio.Cluster.DriveStats`，通过 `Transport.drive_stats/3` / `ShardServer.drive_stats/2` 对本机或 topology endpoint 做 read-only filesystem capacity/free-space 取证，输出 `capacity_bytes/free_bytes/used_bytes/used_percent_x100/mount_root/source/precision/observed_at_ms`，并汇总 endpoint 总量与最小 free-space；新增 `mix maxio.drive.stats [--summary-only] [--drive <path>]`，无 topology 时显式输出 `status=no_topology`。默认 provider 使用 OTP `os_mon`/`disksup`，优先 path/root exact stats，必要时用 cached disk data 做 estimated fallback；测试与特殊部署可经 `config :maxio, :drive_stats, provider: fun/1` 注入。语义说明：这是 filesystem capacity/free-space 观测，为后续 placement/rebalance 提供取证入口；它不是 drive queue depth、SMART、硬件身份、物理吞吐。默认不改变对象路由、write quorum、admission control 或 rebalance/decommission；本轮新增的 opt-in pool write policy 会把该信号用于普通新写的 pool 加权选择。验证：focused `mix test test/maxio/cluster/drive_stats_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/transport_in_process_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/shard_server_io_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_drive_stats_test.exs` → 17 passed；相邻 M8 批次 `mix test test/maxio/cluster/drive_stats_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/transport_in_process_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/shard_server_io_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_drive_stats_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/drive_health_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/drive_identity_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/pressure_aggregator_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/drive_io_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_drive_identities_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_scan_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_heal_fresh_drives_test.exs` → 57 passed；`mix compile --warnings-as-errors` 通过；`git diff --check` 通过；`mix precommit` → 1191 tests, 0 failures, 4 excluded；独立 `Boyle` review 首轮发现默认 provider 在 Mix runtime 不可用、Unix `/` mount fallback miss、malformed provider config 未 fail-open，均已修复，复审 APPROVED。
- **2026-06-13 续作：M8 OS/device-level drive IO pressure telemetry**：新增 `Maxio.Cluster.DriveDeviceIO` 与 `mix maxio.drive.device_io`，以监督树内 fail-open cache 采集本节点 drive root 的 OS/device pressure；Linux 默认 provider 读取 `/proc/self/mountinfo` + `/proc/diskstats`，可从 queue depth、busy%、吞吐、ops/s、平均 latency 或原始 diskstats delta 归一化为 `pressure`。`Maxio.Cluster.IOPressure` 新增 `drive-device-io` / `cluster-drive-device-io` source，`PressureAggregator` peer snapshot 对旧节点缺字段默认 0；`mix maxio.scan` 与 `mix maxio.heal.fresh_drives` 均接受新 source。语义说明：这是对象间 adaptive pacing 的 best-effort 压力信号，不拒绝前台请求，不改变 S3 路由、quorum、write placement、rebalance/decommission；非 Linux、provider 错误/异常、无 topology、stale cache 均返回 0。它不是 SMART、硬件序列号、硬件 service time、物理吞吐保证或 replacement identity。验证：focused `mix test test/maxio/cluster/drive_device_io_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/pressure_aggregator_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/scrubber_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/transport_in_process_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_scan_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_heal_fresh_drives_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_drive_device_io_test.exs` → 44 tests, 0 failures；`mix format --check-formatted` / `mix compile --warnings-as-errors` / `git diff --check` 通过；`mix precommit` → 1325 tests, 0 failures, 4 excluded；独立 `Cicero` review APPROVED（LOW：STATUS 缺本 slice 验证记录，已补）。
- **2026-06-13 续作：M8 read-only rebalance/decommission planning CLI**：新增 `Maxio.Cluster.Rebalance` 与 `mix maxio.rebalance.plan [--current-only] [--decommission-set <id>] [--page-size <n>] [--no-drive-stats] [--json]`。planner 从 PG object inventory 分页枚举当前对象或全版本/delete marker（默认全版本，符合 decommission 需要），按当前 topology 的 `Router.set_for/3` 汇总每个 erasure set 的 logical bytes、data-version count、delete-marker count，并可合并 `DriveStats.sample_topology/1` 的 capacity/free-space 取证；`--decommission-set` 只标记目标 set 并估算受影响 logical bytes。语义说明：这是当前 topology 的只读规划 telemetry，不移动 shard、不改变新写 placement、不实现 MinIO `mc admin rebalance/decommission` job/status/stop 语义，也不处理历史 topology 写入对象或物理 shard bytes。验证：focused `mix test test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_test.exs` → 5 passed；CLI focused `mix test test/mix/tasks/maxio_rebalance_plan_test.exs` → 3 passed；相邻 M8 批次 `mix test test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_rebalance_plan_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/drive_stats_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_drive_stats_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/fresh_drive_heal_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_heal_fresh_drives_test.exs` → 30 passed；`mix compile --warnings-as-errors` 通过；`git diff --check` 通过；`mix format --check-formatted` 通过；`mix precommit` → 1199 tests, 0 failures, 4 excluded；独立 `Turing` review APPROVED，确认 docs 未 overclaim 真实 MinIO rebalance/decommission，残余风险为当前 topology logical estimate 而非物理 shard bytes / snapshot migration plan。
- **2026-06-13 续作：M8 object placement contract（无数据迁移）**：新增 `objects.placement_set_id/placement_pool_id/placement_topology_hash` 迁移（中文 COMMENT + placement_set 索引注释），并引入 `Maxio.Cluster.Placement` 作为对象真实落点解析门面。`Maxio.Storage.Distributed` 写入/assemble 会返回实际 set placement；S3 PutObject/CopyObject/CompleteMultipartUpload、Console upload/create-folder、delete marker 与 lifecycle marker 会把 placement 落入对象元数据。GET/download/delete/version delete/lifecycle hard delete 会把对象行中的 `placement_set_id` 作为 storage opts 传回后端，避免 topology 扩容后按新 hash 读/删错 set。`Maxio.Cluster.Rebalance` 现优先使用对象持久化 placement，并对旧行或未知落点输出 `placement_fallbacks`；`mix maxio.rebalance.plan` text/json 同步暴露该字段。语义说明：这是 rebalance/decommission 的落点契约与只读估算纠偏，不移动 shard、不实现 job/status/stop、不改变 free-space placement；没有 placement 的历史行仍按当前 router fallback 并显式计数。独立 reviewer 首轮指出 Console object detail 删除具体版本未删存储层且未传 placement；已修复为 PG 删除成功后 best-effort `Storage.delete/4` 并补捕获后端回归，同时把 Distributed 未知 placement 从 MatchError 收敛为结构化 `{:error, {:unknown_set_id, ...}}`；复审 `APPROVED_WITH_NOTES`，仅余后续可选硬化：Console best-effort storage delete 失败可补结构化 warn。验证：`mix compile --warnings-as-errors` 通过；review-fix focused `mix test test/maxio_web/live/object_live_show_test.exs test/maxio/storage/distributed_placement_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/placement_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_rebalance_plan_test.exs` → 22 passed；相邻调用面 `mix test test/maxio/storage/distributed_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/cluster_e2e_test.exs test/maxio/s3/multipart_integration_test.exs test/maxio/multipart_test.exs test/maxio/s3/slow_down_quorum_test.exs test/maxio_web/live/object_live_index_test.exs test/maxio_web/controllers/download_controller_test.exs test/maxio/lifecycle_test.exs` → 54 passed；`git diff --check` / `mix format --check-formatted` 通过；`mix precommit` → 1206 tests, 0 failures, 4 excluded。
- **2026-06-13 续作：M8 persistent rebalance/decommission job control plane（无数据迁移）**：新增 `cluster_rebalance_jobs` 迁移（表/列/索引/timestamps 均中文 COMMENT，含 active job partial unique index）、`Maxio.Cluster.Rebalance.Job` schema 与 `Maxio.Cluster.Rebalance.Jobs` context。`Jobs.start_rebalance/1` / `start_decommission/2` 会持久化 planner 快照、job kind/target set、cursor/progress 初始字段与 `data_movement=false` / `data_movement_status=not_started`；`cancel/2` / `cancel_latest/1` 只转换 planned/running/cancel_requested job，不搬迁 shard。新增 `mix maxio.rebalance.start [--decommission-set N] [--current-only] [--page-size N] [--no-drive-stats] [--json]`、`mix maxio.rebalance.status [--job-id UUID] [--all] [--decommission-set N] [--json]`、`mix maxio.rebalance.cancel [JOB_ID|--job-id UUID] [--decommission-set N] [--json]`。语义说明：这是 MinIO-style rebalance/decommission 的持久化运维控制面和取证边界；同一时间最多一个 active job；若计划存在 `placement_fallbacks` 会 fail-closed 拒绝创建 job，避免在真实落点不明时生成可执行假计划；decommission job 拒绝 `current_only`，必须盘点全版本/delete marker。当前不启动 worker、不移动 shard、不删除源 shard、不改变对象路由、pool 选择或 free-space placement。验证：`mix compile --warnings-as-errors` 通过；focused `mix test test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_jobs_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_rebalance_job_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_rebalance_plan_test.exs` → 24 passed；`git diff --check` / `mix format --check-formatted` 通过；`mix precommit` → 1221 tests, 0 failures, 4 excluded；独立 reviewer 首轮发现 decommission + `current_only` 会持久化不完整 job，已修复为建计划前 fail-closed 并补 context/CLI 回归，复审 `APPROVED_WITH_NOTES`，仅余后续可选硬化：DB partial unique race path 可在引入 runner/worker 前补并发竞争测试。

- **2026-06-13 续作：M8 rebalance runner singleton lock + monotonic progress/cursor**：`Maxio.Cluster.Rebalance.Runner.run_once/2` 非 dry-run 现在先获取 job 级 quorum lock（resource `{:rebalance_job_runner, job_id}`），锁内重新 `Jobs.get/1` 并重新校验 job kind/status/destination/topology/PG authority；锁不可得时 fail-closed 返回 `:runner_locked` 并打结构化 error 日志，CLI 输出明确错误。`Jobs.record_progress/3` 改为 SQL 级 monotonic update：progress counters 使用 `GREATEST`，cursor 只有在 `progress_scanned` 严格前进时才更新，stale foreground runner 不能回退 progress/cursor。语义说明：这是 source cleanup / 后台 worker 前的执行边界硬化；不删除源 shard、不启动后台 worker、不改 topology/pool/free-space placement，仍不是完整 MinIO decommission。验证：`mix compile --warnings-as-errors` 通过；focused `mix test test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_jobs_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_runner_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_rebalance_run_test.exs test/maxio/objects/placement_update_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_rebalance_job_test.exs test/maxio/storage/distributed_placement_test.exs` → 29 tests, 0 failures；`git diff --check` 通过；`mix precommit` → 1233 tests, 0 failures, 4 excluded；独立 review `APPROVED_WITH_NOTES`（无阻塞，补 dry-run 持锁仍只读回归）。
- **2026-06-13 续作：M8 guarded decommission source-shard cleanup**：新增 `cluster_rebalance_jobs.source_cleanup_*` 独立 cursor/counter 迁移（中文 COMMENT），`source_cleanup_status` 扩展 `moving`；新增 `Maxio.Cluster.Rebalance.SourceCleanup` 与 `mix maxio.rebalance.cleanup [JOB_ID|--job-id <uuid>] [--max-versions N] [--page-size N] [--dry-run] [--json]`。cleanup 起初只支持 `data_movement_status=complete` 的 decommission job（后续已扩展 rebalance，见 guarded rebalance source-shard cleanup 条目）；非 dry-run 复用 job-level quorum lock（`{:rebalance_job_runner, job_id}`），锁内重新读取 job 并进入 `source_cleanup_status=moving`；每个版本在对象锁内重新读取 PG row，只有当前 `placement_set_id == destination_set_id` 时才通过 `Storage.delete_version/4` 指定 `placement_set_id: target_set_id` 删除旧 source set 副本。PG placement 仍是 target 时 fail-closed 并标记 cleanup failed；placement 缺失或非 destination 时跳过；删除 quorum 失败会进入 `source_cleanup_status=failed`，`--max-versions` 截断保持 moving 并用独立 cleanup cursor resume，dry-run 不删 shard、不改 job；cleanup 完成前会做一次 PG target-placement final guard，若仍有 row 指向 target set 则 fail-closed，不标 complete。取消 cleanup 时保留 `data_movement_status=complete` 并把 `source_cleanup_status` 置为 `skipped`，避免破坏已完成的 copy-pass 状态。语义说明：这是已迁移版本的旧副本清理，不删 PG object row，不冻结新写，不证明 target set 已空，不改 topology/pool/free-space placement，不是完整 MinIO pool removal/decommission。验证：`mix compile --warnings-as-errors` 通过；focused `mix test test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_source_cleanup_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_jobs_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_rebalance_cleanup_test.exs` → 21 tests, 0 failures；相邻 rebalance 批次 `mix test test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_runner_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_rebalance_run_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_rebalance_job_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_rebalance_plan_test.exs test/maxio/objects/placement_update_test.exs` → 19 tests, 0 failures；`mix compile --warnings-as-errors` / `git diff --check` 通过；`mix precommit` → 1243 tests, 0 failures, 4 excluded；独立 review 首轮发现 cleanup cancel 会破坏 data movement 状态、completion 缺少 final target-placement guard，均已修复并补回归；复审 `APPROVED_WITH_NOTES`，仅余已文档化残余风险：未冻结新写，final guard 不是事务级 target-set-empty 证明。
- **2026-06-13 续作：M8 decommission write-routing freeze / MPU placement contract**：新增 `Maxio.Cluster.Rebalance.WriteRouting` 与 `Placement.resolve_write/3`，从 active decommission job 的 target/destination 状态推导写入排除表；普通新写、delete marker、CompleteMultipartUpload 最终对象写入会避开正在退役的 target set，显式 `placement_set_id` 仍绝对优先，GET/HEAD/delete/version delete/source cleanup 仍使用持久化 placement 或普通 router，不被 write-routing 改写。新增 `multipart_uploads.placement_set_id/placement_pool_id/placement_topology_hash` 迁移（中文 COMMENT + 索引注释），Initiate MPU 冻结 upload temp 落点，UploadPart/Complete 读 part/Abort 使用 upload placement，最终对象 placement 仍按目标 object key 当前写路由解析，避免 part 与最终对象被混淆。CopyObject 自拷贝临时对象、S3/PG delete marker 与 lifecycle delete marker 统一传递同一次 write placement，lifecycle AbortIncompleteMultipartUpload 也会按 upload placement 清理 part。语义说明：这是 decommission data movement/cleanup 后的写入冻结边界，不变更 topology/pool/free-space placement，不证明 target set 为空，不替代后台 decommission worker；write-routing DB 不可用时生产 fail-closed，测试环境仅对非 DB owner 场景 fail-open 并打 debug 日志。独立 review 首轮发现 lifecycle marker/MPU abort 丢 opts、CreateMultipartUpload 对 write-routing 错误会 MatchError，均已修复并补回归；复审无阻塞。验证：post-review focused `mix test test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_write_routing_test.exs test/maxio/lifecycle_test.exs test/maxio/s3/multipart_integration_test.exs test/maxio/multipart_test.exs` → 35 tests, 0 failures；`mix compile --warnings-as-errors` / `mix format --check-formatted` / `git diff --check` 通过；`mix precommit` → 1251 tests, 0 failures, 4 excluded。
- **2026-06-13 续作：M8 config-gated decommission/rebalance scheduler（后台推进器）**：新增 `Maxio.Cluster.Rebalance.Scheduler` 并接入监督树，默认 disabled。启用 `config :maxio, :rebalance_scheduler, enabled: true` 后，scheduler 在 cluster singleton lock（`{:rebalance_scheduler, :run}`）下选择下一个可推进 rebalance/decommission job：先推进 planned/running copy-pass（调用既有 `Runner.run_once/2`），copy 完成后在无其它 active job 阻挡时推进 guarded source cleanup（调用既有 `SourceCleanup.cleanup_once/2`）。每个实际数据阶段仍复用 job-level lock（`{:rebalance_job_runner, job_id}`）与原状态机；scheduler 只做编排，不新增第二套迁移/清理逻辑。`run_now/1` 与 `status/0` 暴露给 IEx/测试；`destination_set_id`/`destination_sets`/`auto_destination` 只作用于 decommission，rebalance job 会剥离这些 destination opts 并继续逐对象按 current router/topology 选目标。非 idle 错误会结构化 `Log.warn(action: :rebalance_scheduler, reason: ...)` 留痕，`no_runnable_job` 保持低噪声；错误结果会清空旧 `last_result`。语义说明：这是 MinIO-style decommission/rebalance 后台 drain 的一个受控入口；仍不做 topology/pool mutation、pool removal 或完成后部署配置改写。官方 MinIO/AIStor 文档强调 decommission 会将目标 pool 置于 draining/read-only、读正常、写路由到剩余 pool，并基于剩余 pool free space 自动迁移；本轮只覆盖“后台推进已有 copy/cleanup primitive”这一层，最终移除仍 defer。验证：focused `mix test test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_destination_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/placement_test.exs test/maxio/storage/distributed_placement_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/pool_write_policy_test.exs --check` 通过；`mix precommit` → 1295 tests, 0 failures, 4 excluded；独立 `Newton` review 确认 scheduler/rebalance copy-pass 无语义阻塞，提醒提交时必须包含 pool-write policy 的 untracked 新文件（当前工作区已存在且已纳入测试）。
- **2026-06-13 续作：M8 free-space decommission destination selection（受限目的 set 自动选择）**：新增 `Maxio.Cluster.Rebalance.Destination.select_decommission_destination/2`，基于 `DriveStats.sample_topology/1` 的 filesystem free-space telemetry 为 decommission copy-pass 选择一个非 target set。selector fail-closed：无 topology、未知 target、无非 target set、candidate endpoint 样本缺失、candidate endpoint error、健康候选空间不足都会返回结构化错误；候选必须样本数等于 topology/configured endpoint 数，且所有 endpoint ok，`drive_free_bytes` 与 `drive_min_free_bytes` 均满足 `plan_bytes` 要求，最终按 total free、min free、低 set id 稳定选择。`Runner.run_once/2` 新增 `auto_destination: true`，优先级保持 explicit destination > persisted destination > auto destination；report/日志/CLI 暴露 `free_space_placement`。`Scheduler` 默认 `auto_destination: true`，但显式 `destination_set_id`/`destination_sets` 仍优先，cleanup 阶段不重选目的地；`mix maxio.rebalance.run --auto-destination` 支持前台/干跑取证。语义说明：这是 bounded decommission destination picker，只为既有 copy-pass 选单个目的 set；不做多目的地分布、全局 rebalance free-space policy、topology/pool mutation、pool removal，也不删除 source shard。验证：focused `mix test test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_destination_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_runner_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_scheduler_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_rebalance_run_test.exs` → 28 tests, 0 failures；相邻 rebalance 批次 `mix test test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_destination_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_runner_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_scheduler_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_rebalance_run_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_jobs_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_source_cleanup_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_write_routing_test.exs` → 54 tests, 0 failures；`mix compile --warnings-as-errors` / `mix format --check-formatted` / `git diff --check` 通过；`mix precommit` → 1271 tests, 0 failures, 4 excluded；独立 review 首轮指出 selector 应校验 telemetry 样本数等于 topology endpoint 数，已补 `drive_expected/drive_missing` 与缺失样本回归，复审 `APPROVED`。
- **2026-06-13 续作：M8 pool metadata placement contract（pool-level rebalance 前置）**：`Maxio.Cluster.Topology.parse_and_validate!/1` 现在保留 `pool_ids`、`pools[pool_id].set_ids` 与 `sets[set_id].pool_id`，全局 `set_ids` 改为严格按部署 pool 顺序 + set 顺序展开；新增 `pool_ids(_from)`、`set_ids_for_pool(_from)`、`pool_id_for_set(_from)` helper。旧 RouteB/FS legacy topology map 仅在完全没有 `pool_ids`/`pools` 且 set entry 没有 `pool_id` 时回退为单 pool 0；半新半旧 pool metadata 会 fail-fast，避免 hash 与 placement pool_id 分叉。`Placement.resolve/3`、`resolve_write/3`、显式 `placement_set_id` override 与 decommission redirect 都会在 topology 可用时填充正确 `pool_id`，`topology_hash` 纳入 pool membership，`FS.local_topology/1` 也升级为完整 pool shape。语义说明：这是 pool-level free-space rebalance、pool removal 与多 pool 二级路由的元数据地基；本轮不改变实际 hash 路由算法，不做 free-space policy，不迁移对象，不改 topology。验证：focused `mix test test/maxio/cluster/topology_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/router_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/placement_test.exs test/maxio/storage/distributed_placement_test.exs` → 23 tests, 0 failures；相邻批次 `mix test test/maxio/object_meta_shadow_test.exs test/maxio/object_meta_read_authority_test.exs test/maxio/object_meta_list_authority_test.exs test/maxio/object_meta_list_shadow_test.exs test/maxio/listing_test.exs test/maxio/storage/scanner_test.exs test/maxio/routeb_b6_2_fs_authority_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_jobs_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_runner_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_source_cleanup_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_write_routing_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_destination_test.exs test/maxio/storage/distributed_test.exs test/maxio/storage/distributed_conformance_test.exs` → 125 tests, 0 failures；`mix compile --warnings-as-errors` / `mix format --check-formatted` / `git diff --check` 通过；`mix precommit` → 1277 tests, 0 failures, 4 excluded；独立 review 首轮指出 legacy pool fallback 过宽，已收紧为完整新 shape 或真 legacy shape，复审 `APPROVED`。
- **2026-06-13 续作：M8 pool free-space weighted new-write placement（MinIO server-pool 新写语义）**：新增 `Maxio.Cluster.Placement.PoolWritePolicy`，`Placement.resolve_write/3` 在普通新写（无显式 `placement_set_id`）时可按 pool filesystem free bytes 加权选择 pool，再在该 pool 的 set 列表内按对象名 hash 选择 set；显式 placement override 仍绝对优先，读/删/旧对象仍优先使用持久化 placement。默认 `config :maxio, :pool_write_policy, mode: :hash` 保持既有全局 set hash；运维可通过 `mode: :weighted_free_space` 或 `MAXIO_POOL_WRITE_POLICY=weighted_free_space` 显式开启，`MAXIO_POOL_WRITE_MAX_USED_PERCENT_X100` 控制 99% 默认高水位保护。策略 fail-closed：candidate pool endpoint telemetry 缺失或 endpoint error 会 fail-closed；过高使用率或无可用空间的 pool 会被跳过，直到无可写 pool 时返回结构化错误；可传 `drive_stats` 快照避免每次写入现场采样，当前未内建后台 telemetry cache，未传快照时仍会在写路径现场采样。随后仍应用既有 decommission `WriteRouting`，因此退役 set redirect 只作为兜底，active decommission source set 已从加权候选与 telemetry completeness 中 set-aware 排除。语义说明：这是 MinIO-style server-pool 扩容后的“新对象按 pool 可用空间比例落点”基础，不迁移旧对象、不改 topology、不做 pool removal，也不替代完整 rebalance/decommission workflow。验证：post-review focused `mix test test/maxio/cluster/pool_write_policy_test.exs test/maxio/s3/pool_write_policy_error_test.exs` → 8 tests, 0 failures；相邻 placement/rebalance 批次 `mix test test/maxio/cluster/placement_test.exs test/maxio/storage/distributed_placement_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_destination_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_write_routing_test.exs` → 22 tests, 0 failures；`mix compile --warnings-as-errors` / `mix format --check-formatted` / `git diff --check` 通过；post-review `mix precommit` → 1285 tests, 0 failures, 4 excluded；独立 `Lorentz` review 首轮指出 incomplete telemetry 未全局 fail-closed、S3 错误可能泄露 placement internals、无效 `MAXIO_POOL_WRITE_POLICY` 静默降级，均已修复并补回归；独立 `Archimedes` post-fix review 指出 decommission source sets 仍参与 weighted selection 与 docs 高水位 wording，均已修复并补回归；独立 `Boole` final re-review 指出 write-routing structural tuple 仍可泄露到 Object XML，已补 generic InternalError 映射和端到端泄露回归。
- **2026-06-13 续作：M8 bounded foreground rebalance copy-pass（旧对象按当前 topology/router 迁移）**：`Maxio.Cluster.Rebalance.Runner` 现支持 `kind="rebalance"` job。rebalance job 不绑定单一 `destination_set_id`，`Jobs.begin_rebalance_data_movement/2` 只把 job 置为 `running/moving` 并把 `source_cleanup_status` 标为 `not_started`；runner 在 PG read/list 权威下枚举全版本/delete marker，对每行用 `Placement.resolve/3`（不是 `resolve_write/3`）按当前 topology/router 计算目标 set，仅当持久化 placement 与目标 set 不一致时，在对象锁内重新读取、从原 `Placement.storage_opts(object)` 读源、写入目标 `placement_set_id`，目标写成功后用 `Objects.update_placement_if_current/2` 乐观切换 PG placement。已在目标 set 的行计 `skipped` 并推进 cursor，确保 `--max-versions` 可穿过大量已正确行；缺失持久化 placement 会 fail-closed，不按 router 猜源；`--destination-set`/`--auto-destination` 对 rebalance job 明确报错。delete marker copy 保留 sidecar modtime/generation；对象 copy 保留 version id、metadata/tags/etag/size/SSE 摘要；源 shard 由单独 guarded source cleanup 清理。语义说明：这是旧对象的受限 rebalance copy-pass；runner 本身是前台 primitive，后台 scheduler 已可调用同一 primitive（见 scheduler 条目），但仍不改 topology/pool、不做 pool removal、不使用 pool free-space/write-routing 策略，也不证明完整 MinIO rebalance/decommission workflow。验证：focused `mix test test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_runner_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_rebalance_run_test.exs` → 17 tests, 0 failures；相邻 rebalance/placement 批次 `mix test test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_jobs_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_runner_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_scheduler_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_source_cleanup_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_write_routing_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_destination_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/placement_test.exs test/maxio/storage/distributed_placement_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/pool_write_policy_test.exs test/maxio/s3/pool_write_policy_error_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_rebalance_run_test.exs` → 78 tests, 0 failures；`mix compile --warnings-as-errors` 通过；独立 `Kuhn` planning lane 确认正确边界/不变量（使用 `Placement.resolve/3`、缺 placement fail-closed、cursor 需推进 skipped 行）。

- **2026-06-13 续作：M8 guarded rebalance source-shard cleanup**：`Maxio.Cluster.Rebalance.SourceCleanup` 与 `mix maxio.rebalance.cleanup` 现同时支持 `kind="rebalance"` job。rebalance cleanup 只允许 `data_movement_status=complete` 的 job，复用 job-level quorum lock 与对象锁；每个版本重新读取 PG row，PG `placement_set_id` 是唯一权威保留 set，cleanup 会对当前 topology 中其它 set 的同 `bucket/key/version_id` 调用 `Storage.delete_version/4` 做 quorum 删除。缺失 PG placement 或 PG placement 指向未知 set 会 fail-closed，不按 router 猜；dry-run 不删 shard、不改 job；独立 cleanup cursor/counter 支持 `--max-versions` resume。完成前 final guard 会拒绝仍有缺失/未知 placement 的 PG row。scheduler 第二轮可自动推进 rebalance source cleanup。语义说明：这是已迁移版本的旧副本清理，不是 orphan shard scanner，不证明 pool/set 物理为空，不改 topology/pool，不做 pool removal 或部署配置改写。验证：focused `mix test test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_source_cleanup_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_runner_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_scheduler_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_rebalance_cleanup_test.exs` → 36 tests, 0 failures；相邻 rebalance/placement/CLI 批次 `mix test test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_jobs_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_runner_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_scheduler_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_source_cleanup_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_write_routing_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_destination_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/placement_test.exs test/maxio/storage/distributed_placement_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/pool_write_policy_test.exs test/maxio/s3/pool_write_policy_error_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_rebalance_run_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_rebalance_cleanup_test.exs` → 85 tests, 0 failures；`mix compile --warnings-as-errors` / `mix format --check-formatted` / `git diff --check` 通过；`mix precommit` → 1298 tests, 0 failures, 4 excluded。
- **2026-06-13 续作：M8 topology removal preflight CLI（只读移除前安全门）**：新增 `Maxio.Cluster.Rebalance.TopologyMutation.removal_preflight/2` 与 `mix maxio.rebalance.preflight_removal [JOB_ID|--job-id <uuid>] [--json]`。preflight 只支持 decommission job，要求 job/status/data_movement/source_cleanup 全部完成、destination_set_id 已绑定、无其它 active rebalance/decommission job、PG 中 `target_set_id` placement row 计数为 0、对象版本无缺失/未知 placement row、active MPU 无 target/missing/unknown placement row、target set 所有 endpoint 可达，且 target set 盘上所有 bucket root（含已无 PG bucket row 的 orphan root）、`.versions`、`.uploads` 均无 `meta.json`/`part.0` 残留；bucket `.keep` marker 单独计数但不阻塞。任何条件不满足都返回 `status=:blocked`、`removable=false`、结构化 blockers/examples，绝不静默给出可移除结论。语义说明：这是 topology/pool mutation 前的 fail-closed 证据采集，不改 deployment config、不删 shard、不移除 pool/set，仍不实现完整 MinIO pool removal。验证：focused `mix test test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_topology_mutation_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_rebalance_preflight_removal_test.exs` → 13 tests, 0 failures；相邻 rebalance 批次 `mix test test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_jobs_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_runner_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_source_cleanup_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_rebalance_run_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_rebalance_cleanup_test.exs` → 45 tests, 0 failures；`mix compile --warnings-as-errors` 通过；`git diff --check` 通过；`mix precommit` → 1314 tests, 0 failures, 4 excluded。

参数、`allow_colocated_endpoints`、pool/set 顺序以及每个 set 的有序 `{node, drive}` endpoint 列表，避免只比较 set/pool id 而漏掉 endpoint 漂移。`removal_plan` 记录 `current_topology_nodes`/`proposed_topology_nodes`；`apply_removal_cluster` 的默认和显式 `--nodes` 都会并入原始 current topology node roster，因此 removed-set-only 节点无法被 operator 误跳过；远端先执行、本节点最后执行，任一节点 unreachable/hash mismatch 会 fail-closed 且不记录 cluster convergence。`verify_removal_deployment` 现在必须先看到 `topology_removal_cluster_status=runtime_converged`，并 live-check 审计里的节点名；若审计节点当前不在 mesh/deployment/显式节点集合中，会以 `:missing_expected_node` 阻塞 deployment verification 与 write-routing retirement。语义说明：这是显式、可恢复的 cluster runtime convergence 证明；不写 deployment config、不删 shard、不提供 Raft/all-or-nothing consensus，底层 public cluster marker 仍需经 `apply_removal_cluster/2` 安全路径使用。验证：focused `mix test test/maxio/cluster/topology_runtime_test.exs - **2026-06-14 续作：M8 scheduler run ledger / backoff / alert boundary（自动运维可重启取证层）**：新增 `Maxio.Cluster.SchedulerRun`、`Maxio.Cluster.SchedulerState`、`Maxio.Cluster.SchedulerRuns`、`mix maxio.scheduler.status` 与两张持久化表。Fresh-drive heal scheduler 与 rebalance scheduler 每轮在现有 singleton lock / runner 外层记录每次 pass 的 run row，并 upsert restart-stable state row；结果分类表锁定为 `ok/idle/skipped/blocked/failed`，`no_runnable_job` 不计失败、`lock_unavailable` 与 backoff skip 不计 destructive failure，复杂 blocker/error reason 进入裁剪且 redacted 的 JSON summary，`error_reason` 保持有界摘要。连续 blocked/failed 达阈值后只让后台 scheduled pass 退避，手动 `run_now/1` 不受阻挡；alert sink 崩溃或返回 `{:error, reason}` 只向本次 run summary 注记 `alert_emit_error`。语义边界：不新增 heal/copy/delete/topology/physical 逻辑，不自动启用 scheduler，不 reset job，不越过 placement/MPU/active-job blocker。验证：`mix compile --warnings-as-errors` 通过；focused 初轮 `mix test test/maxio/cluster/scheduler_runs_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_scheduler_status_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/fresh_drive_heal_scheduler_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_scheduler_test.exs` → 51 tests, 0 failures；Noether 独立 review 指出 lock loser 会清退避 HIGH、JSON summary 缺 redaction MEDIUM、alert sink returned error 未记录 LOW，均已修复并补 `lock_unavailable` preserve、summary redaction、returned-error alert evidence 回归；二轮复审又指出 `{:error, reason}` / `error_reason` 结构化敏感字段仍可能泄露，已改为 redaction 后再摘要并补回归；最终 focused 批次 → 55 tests, 0 failures；相邻 replacement/rebalance workflow + fresh-drive CLI 批次 → 76 tests, 0 failures；`mix format --check-formatted`、`mix compile --warnings-as-errors`、`git diff --check` 通过；`mix precommit` → 1507 tests, 0 failures, 4 excluded；Noether 二轮复审 no remaining blocker。独立 Bacon design lane 建议此 slice，明确把生产级 scheduler cadence/重试/告警作为 replacement/rebalance 自动化前置底座。

ledger 不削弱覆盖，scheduled ledger/effective-state 仍由 SchedulerRuns 与 scheduler 入口测试覆盖，cluster-cadence 目标无 blocker/high/medium。
- **2026-06-14 续作：M8 scheduler cadence/overdue observer v1（后台调度活性只读观察器）**：`SchedulerRuns.cadence_report/1` 与 `mix maxio.scheduler.status --cadence` 只读聚合现有 run ledger/state，输出每个 scheduler 的 scheduled-only health、窗口内 scheduled/effective/skipped/blocked/failed 计数、最近 scheduled/effective/healthy run、overdue age 与 stale reason。manual `run_now/1` 不参与后台活性证明；`lock_unavailable` 与 backoff 等 skipped scheduled row 不能作为绿色 heartbeat；latest scheduled `failed/blocked` 不会被后续 manual recovery 清绿；alert/backoff/effective failed/blocked state 优先于最近有 run。未实现 retention/prune：独立 Godel design lane 指出缺 durable rollup 契约前删除 ledger 会削弱生产取证，因此本轮选择 observer 而非 retention。验证：focused `mix test test/maxio/cluster/scheduler_runs_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_scheduler_status_test.exs` → 19 tests, 0 failures；scheduler 聚焦 `mix test test/maxio/cluster/scheduler_runs_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/scheduler_cadence_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/scheduler_peer_cadence_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/fresh_drive_heal_scheduler_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_scheduler_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_scheduler_status_test.exs` → 65 tests, 0 failures, 1 excluded；相邻 replacement/rebalance workflow + fresh-drive CLI 批次 → 21 tests, 0 failures。独立 Gauss review 首轮指出 scheduled failed 后 manual ok 会误报 green 的 HIGH，已拆分 effective scheduled evidence 与 healthy scheduled heartbeat（只有 scheduled ok/idle 可变绿）并补回归。
- **2026-06-14 续作：M8 scheduler run ledger durable rollup contract（retention/prune 前置审计摘要）**：新增 `cluster_scheduler_run_rollups`（中文 COMMENT 自文档化 schema）与 `Maxio.Cluster.SchedulerRunRollup`，按 `scheduler + trigger + window_started_at + window_ms` 幂等 upsert 半开窗口 `[start, end)` 的 run 摘要。rollup 记录 run/status/result/effective/healthy/duration 计数，first/last/effective/healthy run 指针，裁剪且 redacted 的 `last_summary`，`source_max_updated_at` 与 `source_digest`；空窗口也可持久化 `run_count=0`，便于后续 prune 前证明“已审计到无事件”。新增写 CLI `mix maxio.scheduler.rollup --from <iso8601> --until <iso8601> [--scheduler fresh_drive_heal|rebalance] [--trigger manual|scheduled] [--window-minutes N] [--dry-run] [--json]`，以及 `mix maxio.scheduler.status --rollups [--trigger manual|scheduled] [--rollup-limit N]` 只读查看。语义边界：只汇总已关闭、UTC epoch-aligned 窗口，不删除 raw run ledger，不改 scheduler cadence/backoff/state，不启用 scheduler，不移动数据。验证按效率策略收窄：focused `mix test test/maxio/cluster/scheduler_runs_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_scheduler_rollup_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_scheduler_status_test.exs` → 28 tests, 0 failures；`mix format --check-formatted`（当前 rollup 切片文件）、`mix compile --warnings-as-errors`、`git diff --check` 通过；未跑全量 `mix precommit`。独立 Leibniz design lane 建议先补 durable rollup 再谈 retention/prune；独立 Plato review 首轮指出 microsecond alignment、invalid window_ms 默认化、invalid scheduler dry-run 三个 MEDIUM，均已修复并补回归。
- **2026-06-14 续作：M8 scheduler run ledger verified prune（rollup 复核后的明细清理）**：新增 `mix maxio.scheduler.prune` 与 `SchedulerRuns.prune_preflight/1` / `prune/1`。Prune 必须显式指定单个 scheduler + trigger + aligned closed window range；`--dry-run` 只做 preflight，真实删除必须 `--confirm-prune --confirm-scheduler <same> --confirm-trigger <same> --confirm-from <same> --confirm-until <same> --confirm-window-minutes <same>`。`record/6`、实际 `rollup_window/5` 与 prune 在各自 DB transaction 内共享 scheduler+trigger 级 PostgreSQL advisory lock；每个 prune 窗口还会锁定 rollup row 与候选 raw rows，重新计算 count/source_max_updated_at/source_digest 并与 durable rollup 完全一致后，只删除复核过的 id 集合；`record/6` 写 run 前也会锁定覆盖该 `finished_at` 的 rollup row，若窗口已 prune 则拒绝 late raw row。缺 rollup、digest/count/updated_at 不一致、open/current window、已 prune 后出现 late rows、命中 `cluster_scheduler_states.last_run_id` 或 latest scheduled/effective/healthy/latest scheduler+trigger raw run，都会 fail-closed；range 内任一窗口失败则整次 range all-or-nothing 回滚。Rollup 记录新增 `pruned_at/pruned_run_count/pruned_source_digest/pruned_by_node` 审计字段；已 prune 的窗口禁止被 `rollup` 重算覆盖。语义边界：不启用自动 retention policy，不改变 scheduler state/cadence/backoff/alert，不删除 rollup 摘要，不移动数据。验证按效率策略收窄：focused `mix test test/maxio/cluster/scheduler_runs_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_scheduler_prune_test.exs` → 27 tests, 0 failures；相邻 CLI 批次 `mix test test/mix/tasks/maxio_scheduler_rollup_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_scheduler_status_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_scheduler_prune_test.exs` → 12 tests, 0 failures；`mix compile --warnings-as-errors` 通过；独立 Boyle design lane 建议显式确认与 all-or-nothing prune；独立 Beauvoir review 首轮指出 record/prune 串行化、destructive range confirm、latest ordering 三处问题，均已修复。
- **2026-06-14 续作：M8 scheduler ledger retention policy（默认关闭的旧明细清理编排）**：新增 `config :maxio, :scheduler_ledger_retention, enabled: false, retain_days: nil, window_minutes: 60, max_windows: 8784`、`SchedulerRuns.retention_plan/1` / `retention_prune/1` 与 CLI `mix maxio.scheduler.retention --scheduler <fresh_drive_heal|rebalance> --trigger <manual|scheduled> --retain-days N [--window-minutes N] --dry-run [--now <iso8601>] [--json]`。Retention 只计算 cutoff 之前的 UTC epoch-aligned closed windows，并复用现有 verified prune 内核；dry-run 在缺 rollup 时返回 blocked plan / missing_rollups，真实删除必须显式 `--enable-retention --confirm-retention --confirm-scheduler <same> --confirm-trigger <same> --confirm-retain-days <same> --confirm-window-minutes <same> --confirm-cutoff <computed>`，且 CLI destructive path 禁止 `--now` 覆盖 wall clock，API 也要求 `enabled: true` 与 `confirm?: true`。语义边界：不自动创建 rollup，不删除 rollup rows，不改 scheduler state/cadence/backoff/alert，不启用后台 retention timer，不接 heal/rebalance/topology/physical 自动化；缺 rollup、rollup mismatch、protected run、open/current window 等仍由 prune fail-closed。验证按效率策略收窄：`mix compile --warnings-as-errors` 通过；focused `mix test test/maxio/cluster/scheduler_runs_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_scheduler_retention_test.exs` → 31 tests, 0 failures；相邻 CLI `mix test test/mix/tasks/maxio_scheduler_rollup_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_scheduler_prune_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_scheduler_retention_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_scheduler_status_test.exs` → 15 tests, 0 failures；独立 Hume review 指出 destructive CLI `--now` 可把 cutoff 推到未来，已改为 `--now` 仅 dry-run 可用并补回归；暂未跑 `mix precommit`。
- **2026-06-14 续作：M8 scheduler 长跑证据补强（node 参与度 + blocked next_action 入 ledger）**：`SchedulerRuns.cadence_report/1` / `mix maxio.scheduler.status --cadence` 现在输出 `scheduled_nodes/effective_scheduled_nodes/healthy_scheduled_nodes/skipped_scheduled_nodes/lock_loser_nodes/lock_loser_count/last_effective_scheduled_node`，便于真实多节点长跑后判断哪些节点参与 scheduled pass、哪个节点产生有效 heartbeat、哪些只是 singleton lock loser。Cadence latest/window 查询统一限制 `finished_at <= now`，避免时钟偏斜或未来 run 伪造健康心跳。`@summary_keys` 增加 `next_action`，`Rebalance.Closure` report 透传 workflow `next_action`，使 auto closure blocked scheduled run 的修复命令进入 scheduler ledger summary；summary redaction 增强 `--token VALUE` / `--access-key VALUE` CLI 参数遮蔽，继续受 max summary bytes 约束。语义边界：只增强观测和 operator 修复提示，不自动修 PG placement/MPU，不触发 topology/physical/destructive automation，不改变 scheduler 主状态机。验证按效率策略收窄：focused `mix test test/maxio/cluster/scheduler_runs_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_scheduler_status_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_scheduler_test.exs` → 68 tests, 0 failures；`mix compile --warnings-as-errors`、目标文件 `mix format --check-formatted`、`git diff --check` 通过；独立 Maxwell design lane 建议此 slice；独立 Hegel review 首轮指出 future-dated run 会被当作心跳、`next_action` CLI secret 参数可能泄漏，均已修复并补回归，复审 no blocker/high/medium；暂未跑 `mix precommit`。
- **2026-06-14 续作：M8 scheduler long-run evidence gate CLI（真实长跑前后机器可判定门禁）**：新增只读 `SchedulerRuns.evidence_report/1` / `evidence_to_view/1` 与 `mix maxio.scheduler.evidence [--scheduler fresh_drive_heal|rebalance] [--from <iso8601> --until <iso8601>|--since-minutes N] [--expected-nodes node_a,node_b] [--min-effective-nodes N] [--require-lock-loser] [--require-rollups] [--json]`。该 gate 复用 scheduled-only cadence 语义，把健康状态、expected node 覆盖、effective node 下限、lock loser 证据、blocked/failed run 计数、redacted `next_action` 与 scheduled rollup 覆盖汇总成 JSON/text artifact；blocked 时先输出 artifact 再非零退出，便于 CI/长跑脚本保存证据。Evidence CLI 不调用 `app.start`，只在需要时启动 `Maxio.Repo` 读取 ledger，避免触发 root bootstrap、scheduler GenServer 或 runner；报告窗口使用 `[from, until)` 半开语义，与 rollup/retention 对齐。语义边界：不启用 scheduler，不运行 heal/rebalance/topology/physical 逻辑，不写 `cluster_scheduler_runs`/state/rollup，不 prune/retention，不自动修 PG placement/MPU；`--require-rollups` 缺 scheduled rollup 是 blocker 而非 warning。验证按效率策略收窄：focused `mix test test/maxio/cluster/scheduler_evidence_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_scheduler_evidence_test.exs` → 12 tests, 0 failures；相邻 scheduler ledger/status/rollup/retention 批次 `mix test test/maxio/cluster/scheduler_evidence_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_scheduler_evidence_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/scheduler_runs_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_scheduler_status_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_scheduler_rollup_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_scheduler_retention_test.exs` → 56 tests, 0 failures；`mix compile --warnings-as-errors`、目标文件 `mix format --check-formatted`、`git diff --check` 通过；独立 Pascal design lane 建议此 slice，独立 Euler test lane 给出最小/相邻矩阵；独立 Dirac review 首轮指出 CLI `app.start` 非只读、`--require-rollups` fail-open、evidence/rollup 窗口边界不一致，均已修复并补回归，复审 no blocker/high/medium/low；按用户要求未跑 `mix precommit`，留到更大阶段收口。

- **2026-06-14 续作：M8 blocked rebalance closure next_action 映射（operator 修复闭环提示）**：`Maxio.Cluster.Rebalance.Workflow.summary/2` 现在让 `rebalance_closure` 阶段的 `next_action` 依赖 live blockers 与 persisted closure failure：job/data/source/closure/topology/physical failure 优先指向 `mix maxio.rebalance.status --job-id <job-id> --json`；`unsupported_authoritative_source` / `runtime_topology_unavailable` 指向只读 `rebalance.workflow` 重查；`active_job_exists` 指向 active job 的 workflow；object placement 与 MPU placement/hash blocker 指向 `mix maxio.rebalance.verify_closure --job-id <job-id> --json`，供 operator 修复/完成/abort 后复核；只有纯 `routing_mismatch` 才指向 `mix maxio.rebalance.start --json` 创建新的普通 rebalance 计划；clean closure 仍为 `none`。`mix maxio.rebalance.workflow` 文本输出新增 `blockers=`，JSON 继续暴露机器可读 blockers/audit，`Maxio.Cluster.Replacement.Workflow` 默认 audit 透传该 next_action。语义边界：不新增 `operator_repair_hint` 第二事实源，不自动改 PG placement/hash，不自动 abort MPU，不重开 complete job cursor/status，不触发 decommission topology mutation、deployment finalization、physical remove/purge。验证：focused `mix test test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_workflow_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_rebalance_workflow_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/replacement_workflow_test.exs` → 19 tests, 0 failures；相邻 `mix test test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_workflow_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_scheduler_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_jobs_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_runner_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_source_cleanup_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/replacement_workflow_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_rebalance_workflow_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_rebalance_verify_closure_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_replacement_workflow_test.exs` → 97 tests, 0 failures；`mix compile --warnings-as-errors`、`mix format --check-formatted`、`git diff --check` 通过。独立 Mill design lane 建议不新增单一 repair hint、不接自动修复；独立 Pauli review 首轮指出 persisted closure failed 会被 live audit 掩盖的 HIGH，已新增 `rebalance_closure_failed` failure blocker 与回归，复审 no blocker/high/medium。

- **2026-06-14 续作：M8 opt-in rebalance closure verifier / scheduler phase（逻辑闭环证据持久化）**：新增 `cluster_rebalance_jobs.rebalance_closure_*` 审计字段迁移（status/check/verified/topology hash/results/blockers/error reason，列均中文 COMMENT），`Maxio.Cluster.Rebalance.Closure.verify_once/2` 复用 read-only `Workflow.summary/2` live audit，在 per-job runner singleton lock + runtime topology read lock 下把一次 point-in-time rebalance closure 证据写回 job row。状态机为 `not_started/checking/verified/blocked/failed`：只有 `kind="rebalance"` 且 copy/source cleanup 均 complete、target/destination set 均 nil 的 job 可进入；clean closure 标记 `verified`，routing mismatch / missing/unknown/stale placement / MPU blocker 等标记 `blocked` 且不把 job 置 failed，operator 修复后可重试；异常才写 `failed`。新增 `mix maxio.rebalance.verify_closure [JOB_ID|--job-id <uuid>] [--audit-page-size N] [--json]`（会写审计字段，非 read-only，但不移动数据、不删 shard、不改 topology、不触发 physical remove/purge）。`Maxio.Cluster.Rebalance.Scheduler` 新增默认关闭的 `auto_rebalance_closure`，copy/source cleanup 与 decommission runtime/deployment 既有顺序保持不变；显式开启后只为已清理 rebalance job 调 `Closure.verify_once/2`，verified 后不再重复消费。`rebalance.workflow` 与 `rebalance.status` 输出补持久化 closure 状态，同时 `rebalance_complete?` 仍以 live audit 为准。验证：`mix compile --warnings-as-errors` 通过；focused `mix test test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_workflow_test.exs test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_scheduler_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_rebalance_workflow_test.exs test/mix/tasks/maxio_rebalance_verify_closure_test.exs` → 35 tests, 0 failures；相邻 `mix test test/maxio/cluster/rebalance_jobs_test.exs` → 19 tests, 0 failures；review 修复后合并批次 → 54 tests, 0 failures；`mix format --check-formatted`、`git diff --check` 通过；`mix precommit` → 1468 tests, 0 failures, 4 excluded；独立 Helmholtz design lane 建议此 slice，不接 destructive physical automation；独立 Euclid review 指出 failed/checking 会保留旧 closure audit evidence，已改为清空 stale results/blockers/topology hash/verified_at 并补 blocked→failed→retry 回归。
